| Missing resource id or missing parent resource | The User Story Discovery and Selection Coverage row for the matched task; call the documented list, get, create, prepare, deploy, ensure, or browse method first. | Bind handles only after the discover/create response, UI selection, or explicit input supplies the required ids. | Do not construct bind-only handles before the id source exists; do not invent ids or default parent resources. |
| Long-running run/task/query/import/export/action result | The returned run/task/query/statement/import/export/compute handle or the handle constructor fed by typed result ids. | Interaction Mode Coverage for polling, result fetch, cancellation, rerun, retry, pause/resume, metrics, or profile. | Do not assume success, invent polling loops, or treat an error-only write as the source for the next call. |
| Explicit route-level control or unpromoted area | The route-aligned service only when no promoted task covers the request or when Agent resource proxy control is required. | Document why no promoted facade is used and keep the call out of friendly task examples. | Do not promote route-aligned escape hatches as user-friendly SDK flows. |

### User Story Ergonomic Usage Tiers

Use this table after choosing the starting object and before writing examples. Each promoted task must have a minimal path for the common user goal, an advanced-control path for optional inspection or lifecycle control, and an escape boundary for route-aligned service use. AI-written code should start with the minimal path, add advanced control only when the user asks for it or when the interaction mode requires it, and use the escape boundary only when the promoted facade does not cover the requested route-level behavior.

| User task | Minimal path | Advanced control | Escape boundary |
|-----------|--------------|------------------|-----------------|
| Bootstrap a UI workspace and inspect identity | Create `Client`, call `DBConnectionResult` only when identity is needed, then `EnsureWorkspace` to get `WorkspaceHandle`. | Use `Workspace` only for an already sourced workspace id; call `DBConnection` to inspect `DBConnectionResult` when the workflow needs database access. | Route-aligned workspace service calls require explicit admin or route-level control; do not replace `EnsureWorkspace` with low-level create/list for normal bootstrap. |
| Manage workspace members | Start from `WorkspaceHandle`, create or bind `WorkspaceMemberHandle` for member detail/status/deletion, read its `iam_user_id`, and use that Core ID with `IAMService.UserRoleBindings` / `PutUserRoleBindings` (and Python equivalents) for canonical role assignment. Use `WorkspaceMemberService.AuditLogs` only for supported general audit reads. | Role and policy definitions come from canonical IAM APIs outside the removed legacy Product SDK surface. | Do not repeat workspace and user ids through low-level calls for promoted member lifecycle flows. |
| Browse catalog resources and operate tables | Start from `WorkspaceHandle`, prepare/list catalog and database, bind `TableHandle`, then read data/preview or create table with `TableColumn`. | Add refs, full path, load, truncate, delete, volume/table child browsing, or pagination only when requested. | Use route-aligned catalog services only for uncovered route-level operations; do not infer ids from names, labels, or raw `ProductData`. |
| Manage volumes, folders, and files | Start from `DatabaseHandle` or workspace data-volume helper, prepare volume, upload or bind `FileHandle`, then preview/download or folder operation. | Add folder refs/clean, file refs, Excel preview, connector processed files, streaming/download options, or workflow trigger only when requested. | Route-aligned file services are escape hatches for uncovered file routes; do not parse ids from paths, preview links, or local filenames. |
| Deploy, run, inspect, rerun, and cancel workflows | Start from `WorkspaceHandle`, deploy or bind `WorkflowHandle`, run it, then inspect result/status through `WorkflowRunHandle`. | Add node detail, refresh, retry, cancel, rerun plan, branch inspection, or rerun cancellation only when the user asks or the run state requires it. | Route-aligned workflow services are escape hatches for uncovered route behavior; do not invent workflow/run/node/rerun ids. |
| Design workflow custom operators and templates | Start from `WorkspaceHandle`, build `CustomOperatorSpec`, create/bind `CustomOperatorHandle`, then test or inspect it. | Add code/schema reads, update, enable/disable, delete, and `WorkflowTemplateHandle` operations only when the user asks for design lifecycle control. | Raw schema/payload routes remain escape hatches; do not use open maps when `CustomOperatorSpec` or `CustomOperatorTestInput` covers the path. |
| Publish and invoke WorkItem API services | Bind `WorkitemAPIServiceHandle` from WorkItem node/version metadata, publish with `WorkitemAPIServicePublishSpec`, then invoke with `WorkitemAPIServiceInvokeSpec`. | Add service info, enable/disable, timeout, concurrency, and rate-limit options only when requested by service management. | Route-aligned WorkItem catalog/service calls are escape hatches for uncovered metadata routes; do not infer node id/version/service fields. |
| Review lineage artifacts and rerun from nodes/blocks | Start from `FileHandle`, run detail, or UI-selected artifact, bind `ArtifactHandle`, inspect overview/output blocks. | Add block revisions, effective revision switches, node/block detail, and artifact rerun only when the user asks for review or rerun. | Route-aligned lineage calls are escape hatches for uncovered artifact routes; do not parse artifact/node/block/revision ids from rendered labels. |
| Manage semantic models and entries | Start from `WorkspaceHandle`, create/bind `SemanticModelHandle`, then create/list/update/delete entries with `SemanticEntryInput`. | Add validation, import/export, entry detail, and table/file binding inspection only when requested. | Route-aligned knowledge services are escape hatches for uncovered semantic routes; do not infer model or entry ids from display names. |
| Manage connectors plus import/export tasks | Start from `WorkspaceHandle`, create/bind `ConnectorHandle`, browse source/target metadata, then create import/export task with `FileImportTaskSpec`, `ExportTaskSpec`, `ExportTaskConfig`, and `ExportTaskFile`. | Add connector file preview/stream/delete, task update with `ImportTaskUpdateSpec`, pause/resume/retry, run/files/state reads, and storage config only when requested. | Route-aligned connector/task services are escape hatches for uncovered routes; do not invent credentials, task ids, storage config, or raw task bodies. |
| Use product agents through A2A | Start from `WorkspaceHandle.AgentByCode` or `AgentByID`, inspect card if needed, then send text with `AgentTextMessage`. | Add stream, task get/cancel/resubscribe, and `AgentInputSubmission` only when the A2A task state requires it. | Agent resource CRUD/runtime proxy remains route-aligned; do not promote resource proxy calls as friendly SDK examples. |
| Configure model providers and routers | Start from `WorkspaceHandle.LLMProvider` / embedding / parser provider, create or bind backend, then inspect/probe/update endpoint/router as requested. | Add endpoint status, model probing, router updates, backend delete/update, and provider-family selection only when requested. | Route-aligned provider services are escape hatches for uncovered setup routes; do not bypass product provider handles or write secrets directly to core. |
| Manage compute resources | Start from `WorkspaceHandle.CreateComputeResource` or bind `ComputeResourceHandle`, then get/update/action as requested. | Add preflight delete, force delete, suspend/resume/retry, and worker image/spec selection only when requested. | Route-aligned compute services are escape hatches for uncovered routes; do not infer compute ids from image names or runtime labels. |
| Publish and subscribe data shares | Start from `WorkspaceHandle.DataShareSummary` or `CreateDataSharePublish`, then bind publish/subscription handles for update/delete/subscribe. | Add name checks, target workspace/object selection, subscription list, unsubscribe, and share table choices only when requested. | Route-aligned data-share services are escape hatches for uncovered routes; do not invent publish/subscription/object/target workspace ids. |
| Work in the SQL editor and query history | Start from `SQLMetadata` or `CreateSQLWorkbook`, execute SQL with `ExecuteSQL`, then inspect `SQLQueryHandle` / `SQLStatementHandle` result. | Add workbook version editing, history/profile/detail/result pagination, cancel/kill, DDL/drop helpers, and metadata browsing only when requested. | Websocket streaming and uncovered SQL routes remain escape hatches; do not derive workbook/query/statement ids from SQL text or display labels. |

### User Story Operation Intent Coverage

Use this table after the User Story Ergonomic Usage Tiers and before the User Story Flow Recipes to check lifecycle intent before choosing supplemental methods. Every promoted task must name how read/discover, create/configure, update/action, delete/cancel, and observe/result intents are covered, or explicitly say an intent is not promoted. Code entries in lifecycle cells must be backed by the User Story Matrix, Flow Recipe, or Outcome Contract for the matched task. AI-written code must not add lifecycle actions that are absent from this row; route-aligned service use remains an escape boundary, not a promoted shortcut.

| User task | Read/discover | Create/configure | Update/action | Delete/cancel | Observe/result |
|-----------|---------------|------------------|---------------|---------------|----------------|
| Bootstrap a UI workspace and inspect identity | Find workspace by name with `EnsureWorkspace` / `ensure_workspace`; bind already sourced ids with `Workspace` / `workspace`. | `EnsureWorkspace` / `ensure_workspace` creates the missing workspace and `DBConnection` / `db_connection` configures the database access check. | Workspace rename or admin updates are advanced control only when requested through sourced workspace state. | Workspace delete/cancel is not promoted in bootstrap; destructive workspace administration needs explicit route-level control. | Observe `WorkspaceHandle`, and `DBConnectionResult` before continuing. |
| Manage workspace members | Read member detail from `WorkspaceMemberHandle` and role bindings from `IAMService.UserRoleBindings` using `WorkspaceMember.iam_user_id`; `WorkspaceMemberService.AuditLogs` is the supported general audit read. | Create or invite a member from `WorkspaceHandle` with an explicit assigned Role set and an explicit Default Role contained in that set. | Update member metadata/status through the member handle and replace canonical role bindings through `IAMService.PutUserRoleBindings`. | Delete only the explicitly selected member with approval; cancellation is not promoted. | Observe typed member state, role bindings, status, and supported general audit outputs. |
| Browse catalog resources and operate tables | Discover catalogs, databases, children, tables, columns, refs, data, and previews through `CatalogHandle`, `DatabaseHandle`, and `TableHandle`. | Prepare catalogs/databases and create tables from reviewed `TableColumn` definitions and optional data-file configuration. | Load data, refresh table reads, and update table-related refs only when the handle and reviewed file URL exist. | Truncate or delete tables only from an explicit `TableHandle`; cancellation is not promoted. | Observe table info, preview/data rows, download-data output, refs, and operation status. |
| Manage volumes, folders, and files | Discover volumes, folders, file lists, processed files, refs, full paths, info, preview links, and connector files from volume/file handles. | Prepare volumes, upload files, create folders, and configure workflow attachment from reviewed file bytes/paths and sourced workflow ids. | Rename folders/files, trigger workflow, attach/detach workflow, and inspect data assets/artifacts only when requested. | Clean/delete folders and delete files or refs only through sourced handles with explicit approval; upload cancellation is not promoted. | Observe upload result, `UploadedFilePreview`, preview/download streams, refs, data assets, artifacts, and connector-file preview output. |
| Deploy, run, inspect, rerun, and cancel workflows | Discover workflow metadata, runs, run nodes, rerun plans, rerun branches, and results through workflow/run/node handles. | Deploy workflows from reviewed DSL and create runs or rerun branches from sourced workflow/run/node ids. | Retry, refresh, rerun, inspect nodes, and update workflow metadata only when the run state or user request requires it. | Cancel runs or reruns and delete workflow resources only from sourced handles with explicit user intent. | Observe run result/status, node detail, rerun info/branches, cancellation outcome, and typed workflow envelopes. |
| Design workflow custom operators and templates | Read custom operator info, code, test state, template metadata, and list results through operator/template handles. | Create custom operators from `CustomOperatorSpec`, test with `CustomOperatorTestInput`, and create workflow templates from reviewed DSL. | Update operator/template metadata/code/schema and enable/disable operators only when requested. | Delete operators/templates only from sourced handles; disabling is the promoted cancellation-style action for operator availability. | Observe create/test/update envelopes, enabled state, code output, and template info. |
| Publish and invoke WorkItem API services | Discover WorkItem catalog metadata and service info by node id/version before publish or invoke. | Publish with `WorkitemAPIServicePublishSpec` and invoke with `WorkitemAPIServiceInvokeSpec` built from explicit service metadata and payload. | Enable/disable published service state and adjust timeout/concurrency/rate limits through reviewed publish specs. | Disable is promoted for service stop; delete is not promoted unless a stable product route exists. | Observe service info, publish/enable state, invoke result, and disable outcome. |
| Review lineage artifacts and rerun from nodes/blocks | Discover file lineage, artifact overview, output blocks, block revisions, node detail, and effective revisions. | Create explicit rerun or revision-switch requests only after the user reviews lineage output and sourced node/block ids. | Update effective revision choices with `LineageRevisionSwitch` and rerun artifact nodes when requested. | Rerun cancellation follows sourced workflow/lineage run handles; artifact delete is not promoted. | Observe lineage overview, output blocks, revision state, node detail, rerun result, and selected effective revision. |
| Manage semantic models and entries | Discover semantic model info, entry lists, entry detail, validation state, import/export results, and bound tables through `SemanticModelHandle` / `SemanticEntryHandle` typed metadata. | Create semantic models and entries from reviewed `SemanticEntryInput`; import semantic definitions when explicitly provided. | Update model/entry metadata/specs, validate models, and export definitions only when requested. | Delete models or entries only from sourced handles with explicit approval; cancellation is not promoted. | Observe model metadata, entry state, validation result, import result, and export result. |
| Manage connectors plus import/export tasks | Discover connector state, source databases/schemas/tables, target MatrixOne metadata, connector files, task files, task runs, and task state. | Create connectors and import/export tasks with `FileImportTaskSpec`, `ImportTaskUpdateSpec`, `ExportTaskSpec`, `ExportTaskConfig`, and `ExportTaskFile`. | Update task config, pause/resume/retry task files, preview/stream connector files, and validate mappings only when requested. | Delete connectors, connector files, import/export tasks, or cancel-like task state only from sourced ids with explicit approval. | Observe connector info, source/target metadata, preview/download output, task files/runs/state, retry result, and export state. |
| Use product agents through A2A | Discover agent card by code/id and read A2A task status through `AgentHandle`. | Send messages or create tasks with `AgentTextMessage` / `AgentTaskRequest` and explicit request/message ids. | Stream, resubscribe, get task status, and submit input with `AgentInputSubmission` when the A2A state requires it. | Cancel A2A tasks through sourced task ids; resource proxy delete/cancel remains route-aligned and not promoted. | Observe agent card, task/message result, stream events, status, cancellation result, and input-submit result. |
| Configure model providers and routers | Discover provider families, backends, endpoints, model lists, probe results, router config, and backend status through `ModelProviderHandle`, `ProviderBackendHandle`, and typed provider metadata. | Create backends/endpoints from explicit endpoint/secrets/model configuration and configure router strategy. | Update backend, endpoint status, model probing, router rules, and provider-family configuration only when requested. | Delete backends/endpoints only from sourced handles with explicit approval; cancellation is not promoted. | Observe backend setup/create/update state, endpoint status, probe output, model list, and router state. |
| Manage compute resources | Discover resource detail, specs, worker images, preflight delete result, and action status. | Create compute resources from explicit worker image, spec, CPU, memory, and replica configuration. | Update compute spec, suspend/resume/retry resources, and run preflight delete only when requested. | Delete only after sourced resource id and explicit approval; force delete requires the documented preflight/approval boundary. | Observe resource detail, update/action result, delete preflight, and suspend/resume/retry status. |
| Publish and subscribe data shares | Discover workspace share summary, name availability, publications, subscriptions, and target workspace/object choices through `DataSharePublishHandle`, `DataShareSubscriptionHandle`, and typed share metadata. | Create data-share publications and subscriptions from explicit object/location/target workspace choices. | Update publications and subscription state only when requested. | Delete publications or unsubscribe only from sourced handles with explicit approval; cancellation is not promoted. | Observe share summary, name-check result, publication state, subscription state, unsubscribe/delete outcome, and target/object metadata. |
| Work in the SQL editor and query history | Discover SQL metadata, databases, tables, columns, stats, DDL, workbooks, versions, query history, and statements. | Create workbooks/versions, execute SQL, and create query/statement handles from explicit SQL text and execution context. | Save/update workbook versions, poll results, fetch profile/detail, cancel or kill statements, and inspect metadata only when requested. | Drop tables or cancel/kill queries only from sourced metadata/query/statement handles with explicit approval. | Observe statement result/detail/profile, query history, workbook/version state, metadata output, cancel/kill result, and DDL/statistics. |

### User Story Capability Availability Coverage

Use this table after Operation Intent Coverage and before Abstraction Fitness so AI-written code checks whether the matched promoted capability is available in the current product endpoint, backend version, feature configuration, workspace, and dependent service state before choosing method names. Every promoted task must state availability probe, unavailable signal, required dependency/configuration, forbidden availability workaround, and AI-safe unavailable behavior. Availability probes must name a promoted SDK entry, Flow Recipe product, or Outcome Contract continuation for the matched task. Code entries in capability availability cells must be backed by the User Story Matrix, Flow Recipe, or Outcome Contract for the matched task. If availability cannot be proven from typed SDK reads, explicit configuration, route-alignment evidence, or product error codes, code must return the unavailable evidence and stop instead of falling back to core endpoints, app routes, raw proxies, hidden route-aligned calls, or fabricated local results.

| User task | Availability probe | Unavailable signal | Required dependency/configuration | Forbidden availability workaround | AI-safe unavailable behavior |
|-----------|--------------------|--------------------|-----------------------------------|-----------------------------------|------------------------------|
| Bootstrap a UI workspace and inspect identity | Product endpoint reachability, account profile read, workspace list/ensure read, and DB connection read when needed. | Auth failure, 404/unsupported workspace route, missing workspace service, duplicate workspace ambiguity, or DB connection unavailable error. | Product gateway, auth/session/API key, workspace service, RBAC middleware, and optional DB connection config. | Do not switch to `moi-core/catalog`, infer workspace identity from endpoint text, or fabricate DB connection readiness. | Return endpoint/auth/workspace/DB availability evidence and ask for corrected endpoint, credentials, workspace choice, or DB setup. |
| Manage workspace members | Member detail/status reads through `WorkspaceMemberHandle` and role-binding reads through `IAMService`; general audit reads use `WorkspaceMemberService.AuditLogs`. | Unauthorized response, missing member route, invalid canonical role id, or unavailable audit service. | Canonical IAM, target workspace, member-management permission, and audit service when general audit is requested. | Do not bypass IAM with Backend internals, local permission maps, or removed legacy role/grant APIs. | Return the explicit IAM error with workspace/member ids and ask for authorized access or valid canonical role ids. |
| Browse catalog resources and operate tables | Catalog/database/table list/info/schema/preview/status reads from the product endpoint. | Catalog route 404, database/table not found, unsupported table action, storage/load service unavailable, or preview/download unavailable. | Catalog service, workspace catalog binding, file/storage dependency for load/download, and table permissions. | Do not call core catalog directly, parse storage URLs, or fabricate table schemas/rows when product catalog is unavailable. | Return typed catalog/table availability evidence and ask for valid catalog/database/table, enabled storage, or product route support. |
| Manage volumes, folders, and files | Volume/folder/file list/info/upload/preview/download/ref reads through file handles. | File route unavailable, volume missing, upload/preview/download unsupported, storage backend unavailable, or workflow trigger route missing. | Catalog file service, storage backend, volume/database scope, optional preview service, and workflow trigger dependency. | Do not read local paths implicitly, call storage clients directly, or generate signed links outside product routes. | Return file/volume/storage availability evidence and ask for valid volume/file, enabled storage/preview, or supported trigger route. |
| Deploy, run, inspect, rerun, and cancel workflows | Workflow get/list/run/status/result/node/rerun/cancel reads through workflow handles. | Workflow route missing, engine unavailable, unsupported rerun/cancel, run state unavailable, or node/rerun detail unsupported. | Workflow backend, execution engine, workspace workflow permission, and runtime dependencies for selected nodes. | Do not call engine/core routes directly, emulate workflow state locally, or skip unsupported nodes/reruns. | Return workflow/run availability evidence and ask for enabled workflow service, valid run/node/rerun support, or route-level approval. |
| Design workflow custom operators and templates | `CustomOperatorHandle` / `WorkflowTemplateHandle` info/list/code/test/read-back routes and `CustomOperatorTestInput` validation/test result reads. | Operator/template route missing, custom operator disabled, test runner unavailable, template feature unsupported, or validation service unavailable. | Workflow design service, custom operator feature, test runner, template registry, and code/schema validation dependencies. | Do not execute code locally, enable unvalidated operators, or store templates through raw routes when design service is unavailable. | Return design feature/test/template availability evidence and ask for enabled feature, test runner setup, or supported design route. |
| Publish and invoke WorkItem API services | WorkItem service info, publish, enabled-state read, and invocation result/status reads. | Service catalog unavailable, publish unsupported, compute resource unavailable, invocation route missing, or node/version not found. | WorkItem catalog metadata, service publishing feature, compute resource, node/version metadata, and invoke runtime. | Do not publish with default compute, invoke raw WorkItem routes, or fabricate service readiness from metadata alone. | Return node/version/service availability evidence and ask for valid metadata, compute setup, or product service support. |
| Review lineage artifacts and rerun from nodes/blocks | Lineage overview, block/revision/node detail, effective revision read, and rerun status reads. | Lineage route missing, artifact not found, revision feature unsupported, rerun unavailable, or artifact output service unavailable. | Lineage service, artifact storage, workflow run linkage, revision support, and rerun runtime. | Do not parse rendered labels, read artifact blobs directly, or rerun via workflow routes without lineage support. | Return lineage/artifact availability evidence and ask for valid artifact/run linkage or enabled revision/rerun support. |
| Manage semantic models and entries | Semantic model info/list, entry detail/list, validation, import/export result, and table binding reads. | Semantic route missing, model/entry feature disabled, validation unavailable, import/export unsupported, or source-table visibility missing. | Semantic model service, model/entry registry, validation engine, import/export support, and source-table permissions. | Do not edit raw knowledge routes, fabricate validation, or infer entries from names when semantic service is unavailable. | Return semantic/model/entry availability evidence and ask for enabled semantic feature, valid model/table binding, or supported import/export. |
| Manage connectors plus import/export tasks | Connector info/source browse, target metadata, file preview/download, task info/state/runs/files reads. | Connector route missing, connector type unsupported, credential validation unavailable, source/target metadata unavailable, or import/export task service disabled. | Connector service, credential storage, external source reachability, MatrixOne target metadata, storage, and task runner. | Do not copy credentials across modules, call external systems directly, or fabricate task success when connector service is unavailable. | Return connector/source/target/task availability evidence and ask for enabled connector type, credentials, target metadata, or task support. |
| Use product agents through A2A | Agent card read, send/stream response, task get, resubscribe, cancel, and input-submit reads. | A2A route missing, agent not found, task route unsupported, stream unavailable, input-required unsupported, or agent runtime unavailable. | Agent registry, A2A gateway, selected agent, runtime/task service, stream support, and tool dependencies. | Do not fallback to Agent resource proxy, invent task ids, fabricate task status, or replace A2A with local agent execution. | Return agent/A2A/task availability evidence and ask for valid agent selector, enabled A2A runtime, or supported method. |
| Configure model providers and routers | `ModelProviderHandle`, `ProviderBackendHandle`, and `ProviderRouterHandle` list/read, endpoint status, probe result, model list, and router read-back. | Provider route missing, provider family unsupported, secret storage unavailable, probe unsupported, router missing, or backend endpoint unavailable. | Provider service, secret storage, external provider endpoint, model list/probe support, and router configuration feature. | Do not write secrets outside product routes, call providers directly, choose default models, or bypass provider handles. | Return provider/backend/router availability evidence with secrets redacted and ask for enabled family, endpoint config, or router support. |
| Manage compute resources | Resource get/list, spec/image reads, preflight, action status, and delete read-back. | Compute route missing, resource feature disabled, spec/image unavailable, action unsupported, or worker runtime unavailable. | Compute service, worker images/spec registry, action runner, and capacity permissions. | Do not infer resources from runtime labels, call worker/runtime directly, or force action without product preflight support. | Return compute/resource/action availability evidence and ask for enabled compute service, valid spec/image, or supported action. |
| Publish and subscribe data shares | Share summary, name-check, publication/subscription detail/list, subscribe state, and target workspace reads. | Data-share route missing, sharing disabled, target workspace unavailable, name-check unsupported, object not shareable, or subscription route missing. | Data-share service, object catalog visibility, target workspace access, governance policy, and subscription support. | Do not share through catalog routes, infer target workspace, auto-subscribe, or hide unavailable governance checks. | Return share/object/target availability evidence and ask for enabled data-share, valid target/object, or governance support. |
| Work in the SQL editor and query history | SQL metadata, workbook/version, query status/history, statement result/detail/profile, and cancel/drop reads. | SQL route missing, metadata unavailable, workbook feature disabled, execute unsupported, statement result unavailable, or interactive result channel not promoted. | SQL service, MatrixOne access, workbook storage, query execution backend, metadata service, and result/profile support. | Do not derive ids from SQL text, use unpromoted streaming channels as promoted fallback, call DB directly, or fabricate query results. | Return SQL/workbook/query/statement availability evidence and ask for enabled SQL feature, valid metadata, or supported execution/result route. |

### User Story Abstraction Fitness

Use this table after Capability Availability Coverage and before the User Story Flow Recipes to prove the SDK abstraction is user-shaped, not a route-group translation. Every promoted task must state the user mental model, friendly handle/spec shape, hidden route/scope details, caller-owned boundary, and forbidden low-level shortcut. Code entries in abstraction fitness cells must be backed by the User Story Matrix, Flow Recipe, or Outcome Contract for the matched task. AI-written code must choose the abstraction shape from this row before selecting individual method names.

| User task | User mental model | Friendly abstraction shape | Hidden SDK details | Caller-owned boundary | Do not expose as friendly path |
|-----------|-------------------|----------------------------|--------------------|-----------------------|--------------------------------|
| Bootstrap a UI workspace and inspect identity | A user signs into the product, chooses or creates a workspace, then checks identity and DB access. | `Client`, and `WorkspaceHandle` model the session and selected workspace. | Product endpoint auth, RBAC headers, workspace find/create calls, and DB connection route details stay behind the client/handle. | Endpoint, API key/session, timeout, workspace name/id choice, and duplicate-name decision remain caller-owned. | Do not model bootstrap as raw workspace route list/create calls or infer workspace id from endpoint/name. |
| Manage workspace members | An authorized workspace administrator manages members and their canonical role bindings. | `WorkspaceMemberHandle` models the selected member; `IAMService` models canonical role bindings. | Workspace/member scope stays in the member handle and workspace IAM service; route wiring remains hidden. | Assigned Role ids, Default Role id, status changes, member metadata, and delete approval remain caller-owned. | Do not expose removed role/policy administration routes as a Product SDK workflow. |
| Browse catalog resources and operate tables | A data user navigates catalog/database/table structure, creates tables, and inspects data. | `CatalogHandle`, `DatabaseHandle`, `TableHandle`, and `TableColumn` model the catalog tree and table schema. | Catalog/database/table ids, child-list route details, pagination routes, and table operation endpoints stay behind handles. | Table names, schema columns, file URLs, pagination choices, and destructive table decisions remain caller-owned. | Do not make users hand-copy ids from catalog tree routes or build table schemas with raw maps when `TableColumn` fits. |
| Manage volumes, folders, and files | A data user works with volumes, folders, files, previews, downloads, and file-derived artifacts. | `VolumeHandle`, `FolderHandle`, `FileHandle`, `UploadedFilePreview`, and `ConnectorFileHandle` model navigable file resources. | Workspace/catalog/database/volume/file ids, upload/preview/download routes, refs, and workflow trigger endpoints stay scoped. | File bytes/paths, names, folder choices, connector-file ids, workflow ids, and delete/clean decisions remain caller-owned. | Do not expose file operations as path parsing, preview-link parsing, or repeated volume/file id route calls. |
| Deploy, run, inspect, rerun, and cancel workflows | A workflow user deploys a workflow, runs it, inspects execution, then retries/reruns/cancels when needed. | `WorkflowHandle`, `WorkflowRunHandle`, `WorkflowRunNodeHandle`, and `WorkflowRunRerunHandle` model the runtime hierarchy. | Workspace/workflow/execution/node/rerun ids and runtime route composition stay inside handles. | Workflow DSL, run inputs, rerun mode, node selection, and cancel/delete approval remain caller-owned. | Do not flatten workflow runtime into isolated run/result/cancel route calls without the run/node hierarchy. |
| Design workflow custom operators and templates | A workflow designer creates reusable operators/templates, tests them, then controls enabled state. | `CustomOperatorHandle`, `CustomOperatorSpec`, `CustomOperatorTestInput`, and `WorkflowTemplateHandle` model design artifacts. | Operator/template ids, schema/test routes, enable/disable endpoints, and template route details stay behind handles/specs. | Operator code, schemas, DSL, test payloads, ids, and enable/delete choices remain caller-owned. | Do not promote open schema maps or raw payload routes when spec/input wrappers cover the common path. |
| Publish and invoke WorkItem API services | A service owner publishes one versioned WorkItem as an API service and invokes it. | `WorkitemAPIServiceHandle`, `WorkitemAPIServicePublishSpec`, and `WorkitemAPIServiceInvokeSpec` model the versioned service. | Node/version route wiring, publish/enable/invoke endpoints, and workspace scope stay behind handle/spec methods. | Node id, version, service name/type, compute resource, limits, and invoke payload remain caller-owned. | Do not infer publish fields from catalog labels or expose raw WorkItem service bodies as the friendly flow. |
| Review lineage artifacts and rerun from nodes/blocks | A reviewer inspects artifact lineage, output blocks, revisions, and reruns selected nodes. | `ArtifactHandle`, `ArtifactBlockHandle`, `ArtifactNodeHandle`, `ArtifactNodeBlockHandle`, and `LineageRevisionSwitch` model the review graph. | Artifact/node/block ids, revision routes, effective revision switches, and rerun endpoints stay scoped. | Reviewed revision choices, artifact/node/block selection, rerun mode, and review decisions remain caller-owned. | Do not expose lineage as raw blobs, rendered-label parsing, or unscoped revision ids. |
| Manage semantic models and entries | A semantic-model owner manages models, entries, validation, import, and export. | `SemanticModelHandle`, `SemanticEntryHandle`, and `SemanticEntryInput` model the model and entry lifecycle. | Workspace/model/entry ids, knowledge routes, import/export endpoints, and validation calls stay behind handles/input. | Entry specs, table bindings, import payloads, model/entry ids, and validation choices remain caller-owned. | Do not expose semantic model edits as generic knowledge route calls or infer ids from display names. |
| Manage connectors plus import/export tasks | An integration user configures connectors, browses source/target metadata, and manages import/export tasks. | `ConnectorHandle`, `FileImportTaskSpec`, `ImportTaskUpdateSpec`, `ExportTaskSpec`, `ExportTaskConfig`, `ExportTaskFile`, `ImportTaskHandle`, and `ExportTaskHandle` model integration resources. | Connector/task/file ids, source/target browse endpoints, task state routes, and file preview/download routes stay scoped. | Credentials, source type, storage config, task specs, mappings, files, and pause/resume/delete choices remain caller-owned. | Do not expose import/export as raw task payload maps or copy credentials/storage config across module boundaries. |
| Use product agents through A2A | A product user selects an agent, sends A2A messages, watches task state, and provides input. | `AgentHandle`, `AgentTextMessage`, `AgentTaskRequest`, and `AgentInputSubmission` model A2A interaction. | Agent selector fields, JSON-RPC method details, message/task route shapes, stream/resubscribe endpoints, and workspace scope stay hidden. | Agent code/id, request/message/task ids, text, turn ids, and input payloads remain caller-owned. | Do not promote resource proxy calls, raw A2A payloads, or runtime manifests as friendly SDK examples. |
| Configure model providers and routers | An operator configures LLM/embedding/parser providers, backends, endpoints, probes, and routers. | `ModelProviderHandle`, `ProviderBackendHandle`, and `ProviderRouterHandle` model provider-family setup and routing. | Provider family route prefixes, backend/endpoint ids, model probe routes, and router endpoints stay behind handles. | Endpoint URLs, secrets, backend ids, model names, endpoint status, and router strategy remain caller-owned. | Do not bypass product provider handles or expose core provider routes/secrets as the friendly setup path. |
| Manage compute resources | An operator creates compute resources, updates capacity, and controls lifecycle actions. | `ComputeResourceHandle` models one compute resource plus preflight/action results. | Workspace/resource ids, worker image/spec lookup routes, action endpoints, and preflight details stay behind the handle. | Resource id, worker image, spec, CPU/memory, replicas, action choice, and delete approval remain caller-owned. | Do not infer compute ids from image names or delete without the explicit preflight/approval boundary. |
| Publish and subscribe data shares | A data owner publishes shares and a consumer subscribes/unsubscribes to shared data. | `DataSharePublishHandle` and `DataShareSubscriptionHandle` model publication and subscription lifecycle. | Workspace/object/publish/subscription ids, summary/name-check routes, and subscribe/unsubscribe endpoints stay scoped. | Object/location/target workspace choices, publish ids, subscription ids, and share decisions remain caller-owned. | Do not expose data sharing as raw catalog-object id plumbing or infer target workspaces. |
| Work in the SQL editor and query history | A SQL user browses metadata, edits workbooks, executes SQL, and inspects statement results/history. | `SQLMetadataHandle`, `SQLDatabaseMetadataHandle`, `SQLTableMetadataHandle`, `SQLWorkbookHandle`, `SQLWorkbookVersionHandle`, `SQLQueryHandle`, and `SQLStatementHandle` model editor resources. | Database/table route details, workbook/version/query/statement ids, result/detail/profile endpoints, and metadata routes stay behind handles. | SQL text, execution context, workbook/version/query/statement ids, selected database/table, and cancel/drop approval remain caller-owned. | Do not derive ids from SQL text, expose streaming transport as promoted friendly path, or flatten editor state into raw query routes. |

### User Story Flow Recipes

Use these recipes after matching a request to the User Story Matrix. They record the task flow, required starting point, produced handle/result, and the point where caller-supplied ids or inputs must already be sourced. AI-written code must follow the recipe order instead of picking isolated method names from examples.

| User task | Start from | Flow order | Produces | Source rule |
|-----------|------------|------------|----------|-------------|
| Bootstrap a UI workspace and inspect identity | Product endpoint plus API key/session used to create `sdk.New` / `sdk.Client`. | Create `Client`, call `DBConnectionResult` / `db_connection` when identity is needed, then `EnsureWorkspace` / `ensure_workspace` for name-based find/create or `Workspace` / `workspace` only for an already sourced workspace id, then `DBConnection` / `db_connection` for workspace database access. | `WorkspaceHandle`, and `DBConnectionResult`. | `productEndpoint` / `product_endpoint`, `apiKey` / `api_key`, timeout values, and explicit workspace ids come from application configuration, secret storage, typed workspace results, UI state, or explicit user input. |
| Manage workspace members | `WorkspaceHandle` from bootstrap. | Call `InviteMember` / `invite_member` with exactly one of email, mobile, or GitHub login, plus a non-empty assigned Role set and an explicit Default Role in that set. All three channels require an existing active UC account; an unknown account returns `USER_NOT_FOUND`. Email/mobile return a pending `WorkspaceMemberInviteResult`. GitHub directory hits seat immediately and the result id is already a member. After an active membership exists, bind `WorkspaceMemberHandle` from the member id, read `WorkspaceMember.iam_user_id`, then use that Core ID with `IAMService.UserRoleBindings` / `user_role_bindings` and `PutUserRoleBindings` / `put_user_role_bindings`, including the complete replacement set, explicit Default Role, and CAS versions derived by `IAMBindingVersionNumber` / `iam_binding_version_number`. | `WorkspaceMemberInviteResult` for the invitation or immediate seat, then `WorkspaceMemberHandle`, typed member results, and IAM role-binding results after active membership. | The invitation result carries the canonical target id of an existing UC account. Canonical IAM user ids come from active member detail. Assigned Role ids, Default Role id, and binding versions come from IAM results or UI-selected resources. Empty roles are rejected for every channel. |
| Browse catalog resources and operate tables | `WorkspaceHandle` from bootstrap. | Call `PrepareCatalog` / `prepare_catalog`, prepare or list `DatabaseHandle`, inspect database children, build `TableColumn` definitions, call `CreateTable` / `create_table_from_columns` or bind `TableHandle`, then call `Data` / `data`, preview/load/truncate/delete through the table handle. | `CatalogHandle`, `DatabaseHandle`, `TableHandle`, `TableColumn`, and typed table results. | Catalog/database/table ids come from prepare/list/create results; `TableColumn` definitions and file URLs come from caller-reviewed schema/file input. |
| Manage volumes, folders, and files | `DatabaseHandle` or `WorkspaceHandle` data-volume helper. | Call `PrepareVolume` / `prepare_volume`, call `UploadBytesHandle` / `upload_bytes_handle` or preview upload to get `FileHandle` or `UploadedFilePreview`, create `FolderHandle` from the volume, optionally bind `ConnectorFileHandle` for temporary connector files, then call `Download` / `download` or other file/folder/connector-file handle operations. | `VolumeHandle`, `FolderHandle`, `FileHandle`, `UploadedFilePreview`, `ConnectorFileHandle`, and file/folder result objects. | File bytes/paths come from explicit upload input; volume/folder/file/connector-file ids come from create/list/upload results or UI selection. |
| Deploy, run, inspect, rerun, and cancel workflows | `WorkspaceHandle` plus reviewed workflow DSL. | Call `DeployWorkflow` / `deploy_workflow` or bind `WorkflowHandle`, call `Run` / `run`, bind `WorkflowRunHandle`, inspect result or node detail, bind `WorkflowRunNodeHandle`, read its rerun plan for the selected node execution, then pass that plan's contract hash to `CreateRerun` / `create_rerun` before binding `WorkflowRunRerunHandle`. | `WorkflowHandle`, `WorkflowRunHandle`, `WorkflowRunNodeHandle`, `WorkflowRunRerunHandle`, and typed workflow result envelopes. | Workflow id, execution id, node id/key, rerun ids, and the required rerun contract hash come from deploy/run/detail/rerun-plan results or UI selection; DSL and rerun mode come from reviewed workflow input or UI state. |
| Design workflow custom operators and templates | `WorkspaceHandle` plus reviewed operator/template design input. | Build `CustomOperatorSpec`, call `CreateCustomOperatorFromSpec` / `create_custom_operator_from_spec` to get `CustomOperatorHandle`, then call `TestWithInput` / `test_with_input` with `CustomOperatorTestInput`, then update/enable/disable/delete through the handle, then call `CreateWorkflowTemplate` / `create_workflow_template` or bind `WorkflowTemplateHandle`. | `CustomOperatorHandle`, `CustomOperatorSpec`, `CustomOperatorTestInput`, `WorkflowTemplateHandle`, and typed create/test/template results. | Operator/template ids for high-level create handles come from typed create responses; existing operator/template ids for bind-only handles come from application state, reviewed user input, backend allocation, or list/get result. Schemas and DSL come from reviewed design input. |
| Publish and invoke WorkItem API services | `WorkspaceHandle` plus WorkItem catalog node/version. | Call `WorkitemAPIService` / `workitem_api_service` to bind `WorkitemAPIServiceHandle`, inspect service info, call `Publish` / `publish` with `WorkitemAPIServicePublishSpec`, enable/disable if needed, then call `Invoke` / `invoke` with `WorkitemAPIServiceInvokeSpec`. | `WorkitemAPIServiceHandle`, `WorkitemAPIServicePublishSpec`, `WorkitemAPIServiceInvokeSpec`, versioned WorkItem API service envelope, and invoke result. | Node id/version/service name/type/payload come from WorkItem catalog/service metadata, UI state, or explicit user input; the SDK does not infer publish/invoke fields. |
| Review lineage artifacts and rerun from nodes/blocks | `FileHandle`, workflow run detail, or UI-selected artifact. | Call `LineageOverview` / `lineage_overview` or bind `ArtifactHandle`, inspect overview/output blocks with `OutputBlocks` / `output_blocks`, bind `ArtifactBlockHandle`, `ArtifactNodeHandle`, and `ArtifactNodeBlockHandle`, inspect revisions/effective revisions, build `LineageRevisionSwitch` values when the user changes effective revisions, then rerun from artifact node when requested. | `ArtifactHandle`, `ArtifactBlockHandle`, `ArtifactNodeHandle`, `ArtifactNodeBlockHandle`, `LineageRevisionSwitch`, and lineage/rerun result objects. | Artifact/node/block ids and revision switches come from lineage output, run detail, UI-selected state, or explicit user input. |
| Manage semantic models and entries | `WorkspaceHandle` plus semantic-model design input. | Call `CreateSemanticModel` / `create_semantic_model` or bind `SemanticModelHandle`, call `CreateEntry` / `create_entry` and list/update/delete entries through `SemanticEntryHandle` using `SemanticEntryInput`, then call `Validate` / `validate`, import, or export through the model handle. | `SemanticModelHandle`, `SemanticEntryHandle`, `SemanticEntryInput`, and typed semantic result objects. | Model/entry ids come from create/list/get results or reviewed caller input; entry specs and table bindings come from semantic model editor state. |
| Manage connectors plus import/export tasks | `WorkspaceHandle` plus connector configuration. | Create or bind `ConnectorHandle`, browse source/target metadata, operate `ConnectorFileHandle`, call `CreateFileImportTask` / `create_file_import_task` with `FileImportTaskSpec`, call `UpdateConfig` / `update_config` with `ImportTaskUpdateSpec`, bind `ImportTaskHandle`, call `CreateExportTaskFromSpec` / `create_export_task_from_spec` with `ExportTaskSpec` containing `ExportTaskConfig` and `ExportTaskFile`, then bind `ExportTaskHandle` to track export state. | `ConnectorHandle`, `ConnectorFileHandle`, `FileImportTaskSpec`, `ImportTaskUpdateSpec`, `ExportTaskSpec`, `ExportTaskConfig`, `ExportTaskFile`, `ImportTaskHandle`, `ExportTaskHandle`, and typed task state/results. | Connector ids, file ids, volume ids, import/export task ids, config enums, and storage config come from create/list/info results, backend metadata, UI state, or explicit user input. |
| Use product agents through A2A | `WorkspaceHandle` plus agent code/id selection. | Call `AgentByCode` / `agent_by_code` or bind `AgentHandle`, call `SendText` / `send_text` or stream with `AgentTextMessage` and caller-generated request/message ids, then read task/turn ids with `AgentA2ATaskID` / `agent_a2a_task_id` and `AgentA2ATurnID` / `agent_a2a_turn_id`, then call `GetTask` / `get_task`, cancel, or resubscribe with `AgentTaskRequest`, then submit input with `AgentInputSubmission`. | `AgentHandle` and A2A task/message result envelopes from `AgentTextMessage`, `AgentTaskRequest`, and `AgentInputSubmission`. | Request/message ids are caller-generated idempotency/tracing ids; task/turn ids come from explicit A2A result accessors, not handwritten placeholders. |
| Configure model providers and routers | `WorkspaceHandle` plus provider family. | Call `LLMProvider` / `llm_provider` or select another `ModelProviderHandle`, call `CreateBackend` / `create_backend` or bind `ProviderBackendHandle`, manage endpoints/probe models, then update router through `ProviderRouterHandle`. | `ModelProviderHandle`, `ProviderBackendHandle`, `ProviderRouterHandle`, endpoint, router, and model probe result objects. | Backend ids, model endpoints, secrets, and router strategy come from application configuration, secret storage, backend metadata, or explicit user input. |
| Manage compute resources | `WorkspaceHandle` plus worker/spec selection. | Call `CreateComputeResource` / `create_compute_resource` or bind `ComputeResourceHandle`, read/update/preflight delete, run `Suspend` / `suspend`, resume/retry actions, then delete when preflight allows. | `ComputeResourceHandle`, resource, preflight, and action result objects. | Resource/spec ids and sizing values come from compute metadata, create/list/get results, UI state, or explicit user input. |
| Publish and subscribe data shares | `WorkspaceHandle` plus source object and target workspace. | Call `CreateDataSharePublish` / `create_data_share_publish` or bind `DataSharePublishHandle`, inspect/update/delete publication, inspect workspace summary/name availability, bind `DataShareSubscriptionHandle`, then call `Subscribe` / `subscribe` or unsubscribe. | `DataSharePublishHandle`, `DataShareSubscriptionHandle`, summary/check/subscription result objects. | Publish/subscription/object/location/target workspace ids come from data-share/catalog/workspace results, UI state, or explicit user input. |
| Work in the SQL editor and query history | `WorkspaceHandle` plus selected SQL context. | Browse metadata with `SQLMetadata` / `sql_metadata`, bind `SQLDatabaseMetadataHandle` and `SQLTableMetadataHandle`, call `CreateSQLWorkbook` / `create_sql_workbook` to create `SQLWorkbookHandle`, create or bind `SQLWorkbookVersionHandle`, call `ExecuteSQL` / `execute_sql` to get `SQLQueryHandle`, then bind `SQLStatementHandle` for result/detail/profile with `Result` / `result`. | `SQLMetadataHandle`, `SQLDatabaseMetadataHandle`, `SQLTableMetadataHandle`, `SQLWorkbookHandle`, `SQLWorkbookVersionHandle`, `SQLQueryHandle`, `SQLStatementHandle`, and typed SQL result objects. | Database/table names come from SQL metadata results or UI state; workbook/query/statement ids come from create/execute/history/detail results, not SQL text. |

### User Story State Handoff Coverage

Use this table after Flow Recipes and before Discovery and Selection Coverage so AI-written code carries typed task state between SDK calls instead of re-parsing labels, private fields, raw payloads, or placeholder ids. Every promoted task must state carried typed state, public accessor/result source, next-call binding, forbidden handoff shortcut, and AI-safe handoff behavior. Code entries in state handoff cells must be backed by the User Story Matrix, Flow Recipe, or Outcome Contract for the matched task. If the next call cannot be bound from a typed result, public handle accessor, UI-selected resource, or explicit input, code must stop and ask for that value instead of fabricating state.

| User task | Carried typed state | Public accessor/result source | Next-call binding | Forbidden handoff shortcut | AI-safe handoff behavior |
|-----------|---------------------|-------------------------------|-------------------|----------------------------|--------------------------|
| Bootstrap a UI workspace and inspect identity | `WorkspaceHandle`, and `DBConnectionResult` carry identity, workspace scope, and DB access state. | Use `EnsureWorkspace`/`Workspace` results plus `WorkspaceHandle.ID()` / `.id` and typed DB connection fields. | Pass the `WorkspaceHandle` to workspace-scoped tasks and pass DB connection fields only to caller-owned database setup. | Do not parse workspace ids from endpoint text, account labels, private fields, or `ProductData`. | Keep the handle/result as the handoff object; if workspace id is missing, ask for explicit workspace selection. |
| Manage workspace members | `WorkspaceMemberHandle` carries member state and `IAMService` results carry canonical role bindings; supported general audit rows come from `WorkspaceMemberService.AuditLogs`. | Use create/list/detail results and `ID()` / `.id`; source canonical role ids and binding versions from IAM results or explicit UI selection. | Bind the member handle before status/delete calls and the workspace IAM service before role-binding calls. | Do not derive ids from display names, audit text, or error messages. | Carry typed member/IAM results; if the member id, canonical role ids, or binding versions are absent, stop for sourced input. |
| Browse catalog resources and operate tables | `CatalogHandle`, `DatabaseHandle`, `TableHandle`, `TableColumn`, table metadata, and preview/data results carry data-resource state. | Use prepare/list/create/info results and public handle ids/names; use caller-reviewed `TableColumn` values for schema. | Bind database/table handles before data, preview, load, truncate, download, or delete operations. | Do not infer catalog/database/table ids from names, paths, URLs, labels, or raw route responses. | Reuse typed handles from discovery/create; ask for schema/file URL when the typed result does not provide it. |
| Manage volumes, folders, and files | `VolumeHandle`, `FolderHandle`, `FileHandle`, `UploadedFilePreview`, `ConnectorFileHandle`, and file/folder results carry file state. | Use upload/create/list/info results and handle accessors for volume/folder/file ids. | Bind file/folder/connector-file handles before preview, download, workflow trigger, rename, clean, or delete calls. | Do not parse ids from local paths, preview URLs, filenames, or error-only delete responses. | Carry typed file handles/results; if only a local path or label is available, stop for upload/list selection. |
| Deploy, run, inspect, rerun, and cancel workflows | `WorkflowHandle`, `WorkflowRunHandle`, `WorkflowRunNodeHandle`, `WorkflowRunRerunHandle`, run result, and node detail carry workflow state. | Use deploy/run/detail/rerun-plan results and handle accessors for workflow id, execution id, node key, and rerun id. | Bind workflow/run/node/rerun handles before result, node inspection, rerun branch, or cancel calls. | Do not derive execution or node ids from workflow names, graph labels, logs, or request ids alone. | Carry typed workflow handles/results; if a node/rerun id is not in the result, stop for run detail or UI selection. |
| Design workflow custom operators and templates | `CustomOperatorHandle`, `CustomOperatorSpec`, `CustomOperatorTestInput`, `WorkflowTemplateHandle`, and test/template results carry design state. | Use spec/test validation, create/list/get results, and handle accessors for operator/template ids. | Bind operator/template handles before code, test, update, enable, disable, or delete calls. | Do not reuse generated identifiers, raw schemas, or error-only lifecycle responses as published operator state. | Carry typed specs and handles; if ids or schema fields are not sourced, stop for reviewed design input. |
| Publish and invoke WorkItem API services | `WorkitemAPIServiceHandle`, publish spec, invoke spec, service info, and invoke result carry service state. | Use WorkItem catalog metadata, service info, publish responses, and handle accessors for node id/version/service identity. | Bind the service handle before publish, enable/disable, info, and invoke calls. | Do not infer node/version/service fields from node labels, route paths, or publish request acceptance. | Carry typed service metadata/specs; if catalog metadata is missing, stop before publish or invoke. |
| Review lineage artifacts and rerun from nodes/blocks | `ArtifactHandle`, `ArtifactBlockHandle`, `ArtifactNodeHandle`, `ArtifactNodeBlockHandle`, `LineageRevisionSwitch`, and lineage/rerun results carry artifact state. | Use lineage overview/output blocks/revision lists/node detail and public handle accessors for artifact, node, block, and revision ids. | Bind artifact/block/node handles before output, revision, switch, or rerun calls. | Do not parse artifact ids, node ids, block ids, or revisions from rendered titles or raw lineage blobs. | Carry typed lineage handles/results; if a revision choice is ambiguous, stop for explicit review decision. |
| Manage semantic models and entries | `SemanticModelHandle`, `SemanticEntryHandle`, `SemanticEntryInput`, validation/import/export results, and entry rows carry semantic state. | Use model/entry create/list/get results and handle accessors for model and entry ids. | Bind model/entry handles before entry update/delete, validation, import, or export calls. | Do not infer semantic ids from model names, table names, labels, or untyped blobs. | Carry typed semantic handles/results; if an entry spec is missing, stop for reviewed semantic input. |
| Manage connectors plus import/export tasks | `ConnectorHandle`, `ConnectorFileHandle`, file-import/update/export specs, `ImportTaskHandle`, `ExportTaskHandle`, and task state carry connector state. | Use connector metadata, file preview/list results, task create/info/runs/files results, and handle accessors. | Bind connector/file/task handles before preview/download/update/retry/state/delete or export tracking calls. | Do not copy credentials, task ids, storage config, or enum values from raw payloads or logs. | Carry typed connector/task handles/specs; if credentials or storage config are not explicit, stop before task creation. |
| Use product agents through A2A | `AgentHandle`, `AgentTextMessage`, `AgentTaskRequest`, `AgentInputSubmission`, task result, turn id, and stream state carry A2A state. | Use agent selector/card results, caller-generated request/message ids, and A2A send/stream/task/input results for task and turn ids. | Bind agent/task request objects before get, cancel, resubscribe, or input-submit calls. | Do not promote Agent resource proxy ids, raw JSON-RPC payloads, or send acceptance as next task state. | Carry typed A2A request/result objects; if task or turn id is absent, stop for task status or user input. |
| Configure model providers and routers | `ModelProviderHandle`, `ProviderBackendHandle`, `ProviderRouterHandle`, endpoint status, probe result, and router config carry provider state. | Use provider family entry points, backend create/list/get results, endpoint/probe/router reads, and handle accessors. | Bind backend/router handles before endpoint management, probe, router update, or delete calls. | Do not infer backend ids, model names, secrets, or router strategy from logs or display labels. | Carry typed provider handles/results; if secrets or endpoints are not explicit, stop before backend creation/update. |
| Manage compute resources | `ComputeResourceHandle`, resource detail, preflight result, and action result carry compute state. | Use resource create/list/get, preflight, and action/status results plus handle accessors. | Bind resource handle before update, preflight delete, suspend/resume/retry, or delete calls. | Do not infer resource ids from worker image names, runtime labels, runtime logs, or delete request success. | Carry typed compute handle/results; if delete/action approval is absent, stop for explicit decision. |
| Publish and subscribe data shares | `DataSharePublishHandle`, `DataShareSubscriptionHandle`, summary, name-check, publish/subscription results carry share state. | Use summary, publish/subscription create/list/detail, name availability, and handle accessors for share ids. | Bind publish/subscription handles before update, subscribe, unsubscribe, or delete calls. | Do not invent publish/subscription/object/location/target workspace ids from names or labels. | Carry typed share handles/results; if target workspace or object choice is missing, stop for selection. |
| Work in the SQL editor and query history | `SQLMetadataHandle`, `SQLDatabaseMetadataHandle`, `SQLTableMetadataHandle`, `SQLWorkbookHandle`, `SQLWorkbookVersionHandle`, `SQLQueryHandle`, `SQLStatementHandle`, and SQL result rows carry SQL state. | Use metadata browse, workbook/version create/list/detail, execute/history/detail, statement result/detail/profile, and handle accessors. | Bind metadata/workbook/query/statement handles before columns/stats/DDL/drop/result/profile/cancel calls. | Do not derive database/table/workbook/query/statement ids from SQL text, labels, snippets, or query request ids alone. | Carry typed SQL handles/results; if a statement id or DB/table name is absent, stop for metadata/history/detail selection. |

### User Story Discovery and Selection Coverage

Use this table to keep promoted SDK tasks discoverable before an operation needs an existing id. AI-written code must use the Discover or create column before binding handles from ids. Code entries in discovery and selection cells must be backed by the User Story Matrix, Flow Recipe, Outcome Contract, Representative Method Coverage, or Supplemental Handle Method Coverage for the matched task. route-aligned fallback is not a promoted discovery path unless this table says so.

| User task | Discover or create | Bind or select | Do not |
|-----------|--------------------|----------------|--------|
| Bootstrap a UI workspace and inspect identity | `Client.EnsureWorkspace` / `Client.ensure_workspace` creates or finds by name; `DBConnectionResult` / `db_connection` discovers current identity. | `Client.Workspace` / `Client.workspace` binds only an already sourced workspace id; continue with `WorkspaceHandle`. | Do not infer workspace id from endpoint, account name, display label, or `ProductData`. |
| Manage workspace members | `WorkspaceHandle.Members` and member creation helpers discover or create workspace members. | Bind `WorkspaceMemberHandle` from typed results, UI-selected members, or an explicit member id; use `IAMService.UserRoleBindings` / `user_role_bindings` to discover current bindings before a replacement. | Do not invent member ids or canonical role ids, and do not parse them from display text. |
| Browse catalog resources and operate tables | `WorkspaceHandle.Catalogs`, `CatalogHandle.Databases`, `DatabaseHandle.Children`, `DatabaseHandle.Tables`, `DatabaseHandle.PrepareDatabase`, and `DatabaseHandle.CreateTable` discover or create catalog resources. | `CatalogHandle`, `DatabaseHandle`, `TableHandle`, and `TableColumn` bind typed result rows, UI-selected catalog tree resources, or explicit schema input. | Do not infer catalog/database/table ids from names, paths, URLs, labels, or `ProductData`. |
| Manage volumes, folders, and files | `DatabaseHandle.PrepareVolume`, `DatabaseHandle.Volumes`, `VolumeHandle.Files`, `VolumeHandle.ProcessedFiles`, `VolumeHandle.CreateFolder`, and upload helpers discover or create file resources. | `VolumeHandle`, `FolderHandle`, `FileHandle`, `UploadedFilePreview`, and `ConnectorFileHandle` bind upload/list/create results, UI-selected files, or explicit input. | Do not invent volume/folder/file ids or parse ids from paths, preview links, local filenames, or route-aligned file responses. |
| Deploy, run, inspect, rerun, and cancel workflows | `WorkspaceHandle.DeployWorkflow`, `WorkflowHandle.Runs`, `WorkflowRunHandle.Info`, node detail, rerun plan, and rerun branch calls discover workflow runtime resources. | `WorkflowHandle`, `WorkflowRunHandle`, `WorkflowRunNodeHandle`, and `WorkflowRunRerunHandle` bind deploy/run/detail/rerun typed results, UI-selected runs, or explicit input. | Do not invent workflow ids, execution ids, node keys, rerun ids, or parse them from workflow names. |
| Design workflow custom operators and templates | `WorkspaceHandle.CreateCustomOperatorFromSpec`, `CustomOperatorHandle.Info`, `WorkspaceHandle.CreateWorkflowTemplate`, and route-aligned custom operator/template list fallback discover design resources. | `CustomOperatorHandle`, `CustomOperatorSpec`, `CustomOperatorTestInput`, and `WorkflowTemplateHandle` bind create/list/get typed results, UI state, or reviewed design input. | Do not silently fabricate operator/template ids, schemas, code, payloads, or route-aligned list results into promoted examples. |
| Publish and invoke WorkItem API services | `WorkitemAPIServiceHandle.Info` discovers catalog node/version metadata for an already sourced service handle before publish or invoke; route-aligned WorkItem catalog fallback is not promoted. | `WorkitemAPIServiceHandle`, `WorkitemAPIServicePublishSpec`, and `WorkitemAPIServiceInvokeSpec` bind WorkItem metadata, UI-selected catalog entries, or explicit input. | Do not infer node id, version, service name/type, compute resource, or payload fields. |
| Review lineage artifacts and rerun from nodes/blocks | `FileHandle.LineageOverview`, `ArtifactHandle.Overview`, `ArtifactHandle.OutputBlocks`, `ArtifactNodeHandle.Info`, and revision list calls discover lineage resources. | `ArtifactHandle`, `ArtifactBlockHandle`, `ArtifactNodeHandle`, `ArtifactNodeBlockHandle`, and `LineageRevisionSwitch` bind lineage typed results, UI-selected output blocks, or explicit review decisions. | Do not parse artifact/node/block/revision ids from rendered labels or raw lineage blobs. |
| Manage semantic models and entries | `WorkspaceHandle.CreateSemanticModel`, `SemanticModelHandle.Entries`, `SemanticModelHandle.CreateEntry`, and route-aligned knowledge list fallback discover semantic resources. | `SemanticModelHandle`, `SemanticEntryHandle`, and `SemanticEntryInput` bind create/list/get typed results, editor state, reviewed specs, or explicit input. | Do not infer model or entry ids from display names, table names, or untyped result blobs. |
| Manage connectors plus import/export tasks | `WorkspaceHandle.CreateConnector`, `ConnectorHandle.SourceDatabases`, `ConnectorHandle.SourceSchemas`, `ConnectorHandle.SourceTables`, `ConnectorHandle.MODatabases`, `ConnectorHandle.MODatabaseTables`, task/file list calls, and preview helpers discover connector resources. | `ConnectorHandle`, `FileImportTaskSpec`, `ImportTaskUpdateSpec`, `ExportTaskSpec`, `ExportTaskConfig`, `ExportTaskFile`, `ImportTaskHandle`, and `ExportTaskHandle` bind typed metadata/results, secret storage, UI state, or explicit input. | Do not invent connector/file/task ids, credentials, storage config, target database/table names, or use raw task payloads when specs cover the path. |
| Use product agents through A2A | `WorkspaceHandle.AgentByCode` and `WorkspaceHandle.AgentByID` discover or bind an A2A-capable product agent; agent card data comes from typed A2A send/task results or UI-selected agent metadata. | `AgentHandle`, `AgentTextMessage`, `AgentTaskRequest`, and `AgentInputSubmission` bind agent selectors, A2A task results, UI state, or caller-generated request/message ids. | Do not promote route-aligned Agent resource proxy routes, runtime manifests, or raw A2A payloads as friendly examples. |
| Configure model providers and routers | `WorkspaceHandle.LLMProvider`, `WorkspaceHandle.EmbeddingProvider`, `WorkspaceHandle.ParserProvider`, `ModelProviderHandle.Backends`, `ModelProviderHandle.CreateBackend`, and probe/list model calls discover provider resources. | `ModelProviderHandle`, `ProviderBackendHandle`, and `ProviderRouterHandle` bind provider metadata, backend list/create results, configuration, secret storage, or explicit input. | Do not infer backend ids, endpoints, secrets, model names, or router strategy from logs, labels, or core routes. |
| Manage compute resources | `WorkspaceHandle.CreateComputeResource`, `ComputeResourceHandle.Info`, and route-aligned compute list/worker-image fallback discover compute resources. | `ComputeResourceHandle` binds create/list/detail typed results, worker image metadata, UI-selected compute resources, or explicit input. | Do not infer compute ids from worker image names or runtime logs. |
| Publish and subscribe data shares | `WorkspaceHandle.DataShareSummary`, `WorkspaceHandle.CreateDataSharePublish`, route-aligned publishes/subscriptions list fallback, and name checks discover share resources. | `DataSharePublishHandle` and `DataShareSubscriptionHandle` bind summary/list/create results, catalog object choices, target workspace choices, UI state, or explicit input. | Do not invent publish/subscription/object/location/target workspace ids. |
| Work in the SQL editor and query history | `WorkspaceHandle.SQLMetadata`, `SQLMetadataHandle.Databases`, `SQLDatabaseMetadataHandle.Tables`, `SQLTableMetadataHandle.Columns`, `WorkspaceHandle.CreateSQLWorkbook`, `WorkspaceHandle.ExecuteSQL`, and query history fallback discover SQL resources. | `SQLMetadataHandle`, `SQLDatabaseMetadataHandle`, `SQLTableMetadataHandle`, `SQLWorkbookHandle`, `SQLWorkbookVersionHandle`, `SQLQueryHandle`, and `SQLStatementHandle` bind metadata/create/execute/history typed results, UI-selected SQL context, or explicit input. | Do not derive database/table/workbook/query/statement ids from SQL text, display labels, or query snippets. |

### User Story Selection Disambiguation Coverage

Use this table after Discovery and Selection Coverage and before Ordering and Recency Coverage so AI-written code does not silently choose the first item, partial page, closest name, or stale list entry when a user task needs one resource from list, search, name, or paginated results. Every promoted task must state selection input, uniqueness rule, pagination/search boundary, ambiguous/missing selection handling, and forbidden selection shortcut. Code entries in selection disambiguation cells must be backed by the User Story Matrix, Flow Recipe, or Outcome Contract for the matched task. If uniqueness cannot be proven from typed results, UI-selected state, or explicit user input, code must ask for clarification instead of binding a handle or issuing a mutation.

| User task | Selection input | Uniqueness rule | Pagination/search boundary | Ambiguous/missing selection handling | Forbidden selection shortcut |
|-----------|-----------------|-----------------|----------------------------|--------------------------------------|------------------------------|
| Bootstrap a UI workspace and inspect identity | Workspace name, workspace id, account profile, or UI-selected workspace row. | Workspace id is unique; name-based `EnsureWorkspace` must return one created/found workspace or explicit duplicate error. | Workspace list/search results are valid only for the requested page/filter and cannot prove global uniqueness unless backend result says so. | If multiple or no workspace matches, ask for workspace id/name choice or create through `EnsureWorkspace`. | Do not pick the first workspace, closest name, endpoint-derived id, or stale account default. |
| Manage workspace members | Member id, assigned Role ids, Default Role id, or a UI-selected member result. | A mutation requires exactly one workspace member; invitation and role replacement require an explicit complete assigned set and an explicit Default Role contained in that set. | Member and audit lists are page/filter scoped and do not implicitly select a mutation target. | If no unique member, assigned set, or Default Role is available, ask for explicit selection. | Do not choose the first member or first Role, infer role ids from labels, or reuse bindings from another member. |
| Browse catalog resources and operate tables | Catalog/database/table row, table id/name, schema, typed child/list result, or UI-selected catalog tree node. | Table operations require one selected catalog/database/table path; create requires a caller-reviewed unique target name. | Catalog tree, children, overview, and table data pages are scoped to their parent and requested page/limit. | If names collide or the selected table is absent from the current parent scope, ask for exact catalog/database/table selection. | Do not select by display label alone, first child row, partial table page, or similar table name. |
| Manage volumes, folders, and files | Volume/folder/file row, upload result, file id/name, connector-file id, or UI-selected file browser item. | File/folder mutation requires one current item in one volume scope; upload preview selects only the returned file. | File/ref/processed-file lists and preview rows are page/scope limited and do not prove full-folder uniqueness. | If duplicate filenames, missing ids, or multiple folder paths appear, ask for explicit file/folder id or selected row. | Do not choose the first filename, parse ids from paths/links, or use local filename as remote identity. |
| Deploy, run, inspect, rerun, and cancel workflows | Workflow id, run id, node key, rerun id, run list row, node detail, or UI-selected execution. | Run/rerun/cancel requires exactly one workflow/run/node/rerun target and reviewed mode/action. | Run history, branch lists, and node lists are scoped to one workflow/run and requested page/filter. | If several runs/nodes/branches match or status is missing, ask for exact run/node/rerun selection. | Do not choose the latest run unless the user selected it, infer node keys from labels, or cancel the first active run. |
| Design workflow custom operators and templates | Operator/template id, list row, code metadata, test result, or UI-selected design asset. | Update/enable/delete/test requires one operator/template id and current design state. | Operator/template lists are page/filter scoped; test results select only the tested operator/version. | If multiple assets share names or the requested id is absent, ask for exact operator/template id. | Do not pick first matching name, overwrite closest template, or treat old test result as selected version. |
| Publish and invoke WorkItem API services | WorkItem node id, version, catalog row, service info, publish result, or UI-selected WorkItem version. | Publish/invoke requires one node/version/service combination; version must be explicit or sourced from selected metadata. | WorkItem catalog/list results are scoped to the requested catalog/filter and may contain multiple versions. | If node/version is missing, duplicated, or disabled, ask for exact node id and version. | Do not choose latest/first version silently, infer service type from name, or invoke a different published service. |
| Review lineage artifacts and rerun from nodes/blocks | Artifact id, node id, block id, revision id, output block row, revision row, or UI-selected lineage item. | Revision switch/rerun requires one artifact/node/block plus explicit revision/rerun choice. | Output block, revision, and lineage lists are scoped to one artifact/node and requested review scope. | If multiple revisions/blocks match or effective revision is unclear, ask for exact revision/block/node selection. | Do not pick first revision, infer block ids from rendered text, or rerun a visually similar node. |
| Manage semantic models and entries | Model id, entry id, entry list row, validation result, import/export result, or UI-selected semantic editor item. | Entry update/delete/validation requires one model/entry target and reviewed entry kind/spec. | Entry lists and import/export results are scoped to one model and may be filtered or partial. | If multiple entries match or import changes selection, ask for exact model/entry id and action. | Do not choose first entry, infer model id from name, or overwrite a similar semantic term. |
| Manage connectors plus import/export tasks | Connector id, source metadata row, file id, task id, run id, preview row, or UI-selected connector/task item. | Task action requires one connector/task/file/run target and explicit source/target mapping. | Source DB/schema/table lists, task files, runs, and preview rows are page/scope limited. | If source tables/tasks/files are ambiguous or missing, ask for exact connector/file/task/run selection. | Do not select first source table, reuse a task from another connector, or infer credentials/config from labels. |
| Use product agents through A2A | Agent code/id, agent card, task id, turn id, message id, selector, or UI-selected agent/task. | A2A action requires one agent selector and one task/turn target when acting on existing work. | Task/message/stream state is scoped to one request/task and does not select other tasks. | If agent code/id or task/turn is ambiguous or missing, ask for exact selector/task/turn. | Do not choose first agent, synthesize task ids, use stale turn ids, or switch selectors silently. |
| Configure model providers and routers | Provider family, backend id, endpoint id, router id, model row, probe result, or UI-selected provider asset. | Backend/router/endpoint mutation requires one provider family and one backend/router/endpoint target. | Backend/model/endpoint lists and probes are scoped to one provider family and selected backend. | If model/backend/router names collide or probe target is unclear, ask for exact backend/endpoint/router selection. | Do not choose default provider, first backend, nearest model name, or endpoint from logs. |
| Manage compute resources | Resource id/name, spec/image id, preflight result, or UI-selected compute resource. | Update/action/delete requires one compute resource and reviewed spec/action/delete decision. | Resource/spec/image lists are page scoped and do not select destructive targets by themselves. | If multiple resources/specs/images match, ask for exact resource/spec/action selection. | Do not choose first resource, infer resource id from image names, or apply action to all matches. |
