| Review lineage artifacts and rerun from nodes/blocks | Artifact id, node id, block id, revision id, switch spec, rerun target, and action. | Artifact overview, block/revision list, node detail, output reads, and rerun status reads can run in parallel for selected lineage handles. | Revision switch, revision create, and node/block rerun must be serialized per artifact/node/block/revision target. | Do not switch revisions twice, create duplicate revisions, or rerun the same node/block twice while status is unknown. | Return current revision/effective switch/rerun state; ask before duplicate switch, revision creation, or rerun. |
| Manage semantic models and entries | Workspace/model id, entry id/kind/name, import/export identity, and lifecycle action. | Model info, entry list/detail, validation, and read-only export-status reads can run in parallel for one model. | Entry create/update/delete/import/export must be serialized per model/entry/import/export target. | Do not create duplicate entries, import twice, export from changing state, or delete while validation/update is pending. | Return model/entry/import/export status and current validation state; ask before another semantic mutation or data movement. |
| Manage connectors plus import/export tasks | Connector id, task id, file id, source-target mapping, run id, and transfer action. | Connector info, source browse, file preview/download status, task info, files, and runs can run in parallel for selected ids. | Import/export create/update/pause/resume/retry/delete and credential-backed source actions must be serialized per connector/task/source-target mapping. | Do not create duplicate transfers, retry/resume/delete a task concurrently, or run two credential-backed source browses with different configs. | Return current connector/task/run/file status and transfer identity; ask before duplicate transfer or conflicting task lifecycle action. |
| Use product agents through A2A | Agent selector, caller-generated request id, message id, task id, turn id, method, and input payload identity. | Agent card, task get, resubscribe status, and read-only task/status checks can run in parallel for one task. | Send/stream/cancel/input-submit must be serialized per request/task/turn and idempotency request id. | Do not generate a new request id for a retry, send duplicate messages, submit two inputs for one turn, or cancel while input-submit is pending. | Return current A2A task/message/request status; reuse the same request id for retry visibility and ask before duplicate send/cancel/input. |
| Configure model providers and routers | Provider family, backend id, endpoint id, router id, model list/probe target, secret identity, and lifecycle action. | Provider/backend/router reads, endpoint status, and model list reads can run in parallel after provider family is selected. | Backend create/update/delete, endpoint status, probe, secret update, and router update must be serialized per provider/backend/router. | Do not probe twice, rotate/update secrets concurrently, change routers from two workers, or delete during endpoint update. | Return current backend/router/probe status and selected ids; ask before duplicate external probe, secret, router, or lifecycle action. |
| Manage compute resources | Resource id, spec/image id, action id/type, sizing tuple window, and delete/preflight target. | Resource detail, spec/image reads, and preflight reads can run in parallel for one selected resource. | Create/update/resize/suspend/resume/retry/delete/force-delete must be serialized per resource or resource name/spec. | Do not create duplicate compute, resize while action is pending, submit conflicting suspend/resume, or force-delete twice. | Return current resource/action/preflight status and selected resource identity; ask before duplicate capacity or destructive action. |
| Publish and subscribe data shares | Publish id, subscription id, object id, target workspace id, name-check value, and share action. | Summary, name-check, publication/subscription detail, and list reads can run in parallel for the selected target. | Publish/update/delete/subscribe/unsubscribe must be serialized per object/target/publish/subscription id. | Do not publish or subscribe twice, delete while subscribe is pending, or run conflicting unsubscribe/delete actions. | Return current share/subscription/name-check status and target identity; ask before duplicate or conflicting share action. |
| Work in the SQL editor and query history | Workbook id, version id, query id, statement id, SQL text hash, database/table scope, and action type. | Metadata, workbook/version, history, query status, statement result/detail/profile reads can run in parallel for selected handles. | Execute, rerun, cancel/kill, drop/DDL/DML, workbook update/delete must be serialized per workbook/query/statement/database target. | Do not execute the same SQL twice, cancel/kill while execute status is unknown, or submit concurrent DDL/drop from one request. | Return current query/statement/workbook status and SQL scope; ask before duplicate execution, cancel/kill, or destructive SQL mutation. |

### User Story Impact Assessment Coverage

Use this table after Concurrent Submission Coverage and before Confirmation and Preview Coverage so AI-written code can explain likely impact before asking for approval or executing high-impact SDK operations. Every promoted task must state impact source, affected scope, cost/external side effects, uncertainty boundary, and AI-safe impact summary. Code entries in impact assessment cells must be backed by the User Story Matrix, Flow Recipe, or Outcome Contract for the matched task. If impact cannot be estimated from typed SDK state, code must say which source is missing and stop before confirmation or execution instead of guessing the blast radius.

| User task | Impact source | Affected scope | Cost/external side effects | Uncertainty boundary | AI-safe impact summary |
|-----------|---------------|----------------|----------------------------|----------------------|------------------------|
| Bootstrap a UI workspace and inspect identity | Account profile, workspace list/read, duplicate-name evidence, and DB connection state. | Current account, selected workspace id/name, membership, and optional DB visibility. | Workspace creation changes account state; DB detail exposure may reveal infrastructure metadata. | Unknown auth, duplicate names, missing endpoint, or hidden workspace membership means impact is not estimable. | Summarize account/workspace scope, create-or-bind impact, missing state, and ask before create/switch/exposure. |
| Manage workspace members | Member detail, current canonical role bindings, status, and supported general audit rows. | The selected member, role ids, workspace access, and IAM audit trail. | Binding, status, and delete changes can immediately alter access and create audit history. | Missing binding state, ambiguous member, denied detail, or stale status makes impact uncertain. | Summarize current versus requested member state, affected ids, audit impact, and unknowns. |
| Browse catalog resources and operate tables | Catalog/database/table info, schema, preview/data page, refs, load status, and table preflight read. | Table path/id, schema, refs, preview/download exposure, load target, and data rows. | Load/download/truncate/delete can consume storage/compute, expose data, or destroy table data. | Missing schema/status, stale preview, unknown refs, or hidden row counts means blast radius is uncertain. | Summarize table target, operation type, data exposure/destruction risk, and missing proof before approval. |
| Manage volumes, folders, and files | Volume/folder/file info, upload preview, refs, download/preview state, and workflow trigger target. | File/folder path/id, refs, signed links, storage object, and triggered workflow. | Uploads consume storage; downloads expose data; triggers can start workflow compute or external processing. | Missing refs, stale file info, unknown link visibility, or unknown workflow target means impact is uncertain. | Summarize storage/link/workflow impact, scoped file ids, and unknown refs before mutation or exposure. |
| Deploy, run, inspect, rerun, and cancel workflows | Workflow detail, input summary, run/node/rerun status, branch state, and cancel/rerun plan. | Workflow definition, run id, node key, branch/rerun target, outputs, and external node calls. | Runs/reruns consume compute and may call external systems; cancel/delete changes execution visibility. | Missing current run state, hidden inputs, unknown node side effects, or stale branch state means impact is uncertain. | Summarize run/rerun/cancel target, compute/external risk, output visibility, and missing execution proof. |
| Design workflow custom operators and templates | Operator/template detail, code/schema validation, test input summary, enabled state, and template info. | Operator/template id, code/schema, test payload, enabled workflows, and template consumers. | Tests execute code; enable/update/delete can change workflow design behavior. | Missing validation, unknown template users, hidden code, or stale enabled state means impact is uncertain. | Summarize design target, code/test/lifecycle impact, consumers if known, and unknown validation state. |
| Publish and invoke WorkItem API services | Service info, node/version detail, publish spec, invoke spec, enabled state, and limit/status reads. | Node/version/service id, published endpoint, payload, limits, and invocation result. | Publish changes availability; invoke spends compute/API quota and may call service dependencies. | Missing service status, unknown payload effect, hidden limits, or stale version state means impact is uncertain. | Summarize service lifecycle/invoke impact, payload scope, quota/limit risk, and missing service proof. |
| Review lineage artifacts and rerun from nodes/blocks | Artifact overview, node/block/revision detail, effective revision, output summary, and rerun plan. | Artifact id, node id, block id, revision id, visible output, rerun branch, and downstream consumers. | Rerun consumes workflow compute; revision switch changes which output users see. | Missing effective revision, hidden output content, unknown consumers, or stale branch state means impact is uncertain. | Summarize selected lineage target, output visibility, rerun cost, and unknown revision/consumer state. |
| Manage semantic models and entries | Model/entry detail, validation result, import/export result, list/search result, and lifecycle read-back. | Model/entry ids, table bindings, business terms, import/export payloads, and consumers. | Import/export moves semantic data; validation may spend backend compute; delete/update changes semantic behavior. | Missing validation, hidden bindings, stale entry list, or unknown consumers means impact is uncertain. | Summarize semantic target, validation/import/export/delete scope, consumer risk, and missing proof. |
| Manage connectors plus import/export tasks | Connector/source schema, credential validation, MO target check, file preview, task state, runs, and files. | Connector, credentials boundary, source/target systems, mappings, task/files/runs, and target tables. | Transfers move data, use credentials, consume storage/compute, and may call external systems. | Missing credential validation, unknown source schema, stale task state, or hidden target mapping means impact is uncertain. | Summarize source-to-target transfer scope, credential/external risk, task state, and missing mapping proof. |
| Use product agents through A2A | Agent card, request/message summary, task state, input-required turn, stream/resubscribe state, and cancel target. | Agent selector, request/message/task ids, prompt/input content, stream output, and tool calls. | Sends/streams spend model/tool/API quota and may trigger agent tools or external actions. | Missing agent card, duplicate request id, hidden tool policy, or stale task state means impact is uncertain. | Summarize agent/task/message scope, quota/tool risk, current state, and missing selector/task proof. |
| Configure model providers and routers | Provider/backend/router detail, endpoint validation, probe result, model list, and redacted secret state. | Provider family, backend/router ids, endpoint/model routing, secrets, and probe target. | Probes may call external providers; router/secret changes affect model traffic. | Missing endpoint validation, hidden secrets, stale router state, or unknown provider error means impact is uncertain. | Summarize provider/router target, external probe/routing impact, secret boundary, and missing validation proof. |
| Manage compute resources | Resource detail, spec/image reads, preflight result, action status window, and capacity/cost state. | Resource id/name, spec/image, replicas/capacity, action/delete target, and workloads. | Create/update/action/delete changes capacity, availability, runtime cost, and workload behavior. | Missing preflight, stale, unknown workloads, hidden specs, or in-flight action means impact is uncertain. | Summarize resource/action cost/capacity impact, workload risk if known, and missing preflight proof. |
| Publish and subscribe data shares | Workspace summary, name-check, publish/subscription detail, object read, target workspace list, and governance state. | Shared object, publish/subscription ids, target workspace/location, subscribers, and governance/audit. | Sharing can expose data across workspaces and changes governance/audit state. | Missing target workspace proof, stale name-check, hidden subscribers, or unknown object scope means impact is uncertain. | Summarize object/target exposure, governance impact, subscriber scope, and missing target proof. |
| Work in the SQL editor and query history | SQL text review, metadata, query/statement status, history, workbook/version detail, result/profile, and cancel/drop read-back. | Database/table/workbook/query/statement ids, SQL operation type, rows/results, profile, and history. | SQL can consume database resources; DDL/DML/drop/kill can mutate or destroy data. | Missing metadata, hidden SQL text, stale query state, unknown transaction proof, or unbounded result limit means impact is uncertain. | Summarize SQL operation, target database/table, result/destructive risk, limits, and missing metadata/status proof. |

### User Story Confirmation and Preview Coverage

Use this table after Impact Assessment Coverage and before Abort and Cleanup Coverage so AI-written code asks for explicit confirmation before destructive, cost-incurring, external, data-moving, permission-changing, or irreversible SDK operations instead of treating broad user intent, stale previews, or inferred defaults as approval. Every promoted task must state confirmation trigger, preview/preflight source, user-visible confirmation summary, forbidden implicit approval, and AI-safe confirmation behavior. Code entries in confirmation and preview cells must be backed by the User Story Matrix, Flow Recipe, or Outcome Contract for the matched task. If confirmation cannot be proven, code must return the preview/preflight result and stop instead of executing the action.

| User task | Confirmation trigger | Preview/preflight source | User-visible confirmation summary | Forbidden implicit approval | AI-safe confirmation behavior |
|-----------|----------------------|--------------------------|-----------------------------------|-----------------------------|-------------------------------|
| Bootstrap a UI workspace and inspect identity | Confirm before workspace create, workspace switch, or exposing DB connection details. | Account profile, workspace list/read result, duplicate-name check, and DB connection read. | Show endpoint/auth mode, workspace id/name, create-or-bind decision, duplicate count, and DB detail visibility. | Do not assume a default workspace, infer create approval from endpoint text, or bind an ambiguous name. | Return the lookup/preflight result and ask for explicit approval when create, switch, ambiguity, or DB exposure is involved. |
| Manage workspace members | Confirm before invitation/create, role-binding replacement, status changes, or member deletion. | Member detail, current bindings, status, and supported general audit state. | Show member id, current versus requested bindings/status, and access/audit impact. | Do not infer approval from a broad request, reuse another member's bindings, or delete without a reviewed target. | Show the IAM member diff and require explicit approval for access-changing or destructive calls. |
| Browse catalog resources and operate tables | Confirm before create/load/truncate/delete/download-link exposure or other data-moving table operations. | Catalog/database/table info, schema, preview/data page, refs, load status, and table preflight read. | Show table path/id, schema summary, preview/page counts, refs, and destructive or load target. | Do not execute load/truncate/delete from guessed names, stale preview rows, or display labels. | Return table preview/preflight evidence and ask before destructive, data-moving, or link-exposing actions. |
| Manage volumes, folders, and files | Confirm before upload replacement, delete/clean, exposing signed links, or triggering workflows from files. | Volume/folder/file info, upload preview, refs, download/preview state, and workflow trigger target. | Show path/name/id, size or preview summary, ref count, replacement/delete scope, and workflow target. | Do not infer approval to delete, clean, replace uploads, expose links, or trigger workflows from a general file request. | Show file/folder preflight details and ask before storage-changing, link-exposing, or workflow-triggering actions. |
| Deploy, run, inspect, rerun, and cancel workflows | Confirm before deploy, run, rerun, cancel, delete, or changing workflow execution inputs. | Workflow detail, input summary, run/node/rerun status, branch state, and rerun/cancel preflight plan. | Show workflow/run/node ids, input summary, current status, compute/external side effects, and cancel/rerun target. | Do not treat "go ahead" without a specific handle/status as approval to run, rerun, cancel, or deploy. | Return current workflow/run state and ask for explicit approval before execution-changing actions. |
| Design workflow custom operators and templates | Confirm before executing tests, publishing/enabling code, updating schemas/templates, disabling, or deleting. | Operator/template detail, code/schema validation, test input summary, enabled state, and template info. | Show operator/template id/name, code or schema summary, test payload summary, lifecycle action, and enabled/delete impact. | Do not enable unreviewed code, rerun tests, overwrite schemas, or delete templates from inferred intent. | Show validation/test preflight and ask before code execution or lifecycle changes. |
| Publish and invoke WorkItem API services | Confirm before publish, enable/disable, invoke with payload, or changing service limits. | Service info, node/version detail, publish spec, invoke spec, enabled state, and limit/status reads. | Show node/version/service ids, payload summary, publish/enabled state, timeout/concurrency limits, and cost boundary. | Do not publish, enable, disable, or invoke from inferred payloads, stale service state, or implicit retry intent. | Return service preview/status and ask before publish, lifecycle, or invocation calls. |
| Review lineage artifacts and rerun from nodes/blocks | Confirm before revision switch, rerun from node/block, or exposing artifact output blocks. | Artifact overview, node/block/revision detail, effective revision read, output summary, and rerun preflight plan. | Show artifact/node/block/revision ids, effective output summary, selected switch, and rerun cost/branch target. | Do not switch revisions, expose raw outputs, or rerun from guessed node/block ids. | Show lineage and rerun preflight evidence, then ask before switch, exposure, or rerun. |
| Manage semantic models and entries | Confirm before create/update/delete/import/export entries or models. | Model/entry detail, validation result, import/export spec, list/search result, and delete/update preflight. | Show model/entry ids, entry kind/name, validation summary, import/export scope, and destructive/update impact. | Do not overwrite, delete, import, or export semantic data from broad requests or stale validation. | Return validation/preflight result and ask before destructive or data-moving semantic changes. |
| Manage connectors plus import/export tasks | Confirm before connector create/update/delete, credential use, import/export start, retry/resume/pause/delete, or external transfer. | Connector/source schema, credential validation, MO target check, file preview, task state, and import/export preflight. | Show connector/task/file ids, source and target summary, mapping, credential boundary, external systems, and task action. | Do not transfer data, retry/resume/delete tasks, or use credentials from inferred config or stale task state. | Show connector/task preflight and ask before credential-sensitive, external, or data-moving actions. |
| Use product agents through A2A | Confirm before send/stream, duplicate request retry, cancel, or input-submit when the turn content is user-owned. | Agent card, request/message summary, task state, input-required turn, resubscribe state, and cancel target. | Show agent selector, request/message/task ids, text summary, method, current task/input turn, and tool/cost boundary. | Do not send duplicate messages, invent input answers, stream with a guessed agent, or cancel from stale task state. | Reuse reviewed ids, return task preview/state, and ask before send/stream/cancel/input-submit when intent is not explicit. |
| Configure model providers and routers | Confirm before create/update/delete backends, probes, secret updates, router strategy changes, or model routing changes. | Provider/backend/router detail, endpoint validation, probe result, model list, and secret redaction check. | Show provider family, backend/router ids, endpoint/model summary, router action, probe target, and redacted secret boundary. | Do not probe external endpoints, change routers, update secrets, or delete backends from inferred model names. | Show provider/router preflight with secrets redacted and ask before external, secret, or routing changes. |
| Manage compute resources | Confirm before create/update/resize/suspend/resume/retry/delete/force-delete or choosing specs/images. | Resource detail, spec/image reads, preflight-delete result window, action status, and capacity/cost state. | Show resource id/name, spec/image summary, action, cost/capacity impact window, and preflight warnings. | Do not force delete, resize, suspend/resume, or create duplicate compute resources from inferred capacity needs. | Return resource/preflight evidence and ask before cost, capacity, or destructive actions. |
| Publish and subscribe data shares | Confirm before publish, subscribe, unsubscribe, delete, target workspace selection, or governance-sensitive exposure. | Workspace summary, name-check, publish/subscription detail, object read, target workspace list, and governance state. | Show object id/name, target workspace/location, share/subscription ids, name availability, and governance impact. | Do not publish, subscribe, unsubscribe, or delete shares from broad workspace names or inferred object selection. | Show share preview/name-check and ask before cross-workspace or governance-changing actions. |
| Work in the SQL editor and query history | Confirm before execute, rerun, cancel, kill, drop, DDL/DML, workbook delete/update, or exposing SQL/results/profile. | SQL text review, metadata table info, query/statement status, history, workbook/version detail, and result/profile preview. | Show SQL summary, database/table/workbook ids, operation type, result limit, destructive warning, and cancel/drop target. | Do not execute, rewrite, drop, kill, or cancel SQL from inferred text, display labels, or stale query ids. | Return SQL/metadata preview and ask before execution, destructive database operations, or result exposure. |

### User Story Abort and Cleanup Coverage

Use this table after Confirmation and Preview Coverage and before Progress Visibility Coverage so AI-written code handles declined confirmations, user-aborted flows, partial writes, and failed multi-step workflows without pretending the SDK can rollback product or external side effects. Every promoted task must state abort trigger, cleanup/compensation target, state proof before cleanup, forbidden rollback shortcut, and AI-safe abort behavior. Code entries in abort and cleanup cells must be backed by the User Story Matrix, Flow Recipe, or Outcome Contract for the matched task. If cleanup/compensation cannot be proven safe and scoped, code must return the last typed handle/status/result plus explicit remaining side effects and stop instead of deleting or retrying automatically.

| User task | Abort trigger | Cleanup/compensation target | State proof before cleanup | Forbidden rollback shortcut | AI-safe abort behavior |
|-----------|---------------|-----------------------------|----------------------------|-----------------------------|------------------------|
| Bootstrap a UI workspace and inspect identity | User rejects workspace create/switch, auth fails, duplicate names appear, or DB detail exposure is declined. | Newly created workspace only when its handle came from the current create result; otherwise no cleanup is promoted. | Account/workspace list, create result, duplicate-name evidence, and DB connection read state. | Do not delete an existing workspace, clear credentials, or claim auth/workspace selection was rolled back. | Return the current account/workspace evidence, keep credentials caller-owned, and ask the user to choose or delete explicitly. |
| Manage workspace members | The user declines a binding/status/delete action, a mutation fails, or the member target becomes ambiguous. | Only the exact member change represented by the current handle and read-back state. | Member detail, bindings, status read-back, and supported general audit results. | Do not broaden compensation, delete related identities, or fabricate an audit rollback. | Return the member, attempted action, and read-back evidence; require explicit follow-up for compensation. |
| Browse catalog resources and operate tables | User aborts load/create/truncate/delete/download exposure, load fails, or table target becomes stale. | Current create/load job or table operation handle when available; data destruction is not automatically compensable. | Table info/status, schema, preview/data page, refs, load status, and delete/truncate read-back. | Do not recreate rows, restore truncated data, delete adjacent tables, or hide partial load artifacts. | Return table handle/status plus remaining data side effects; ask before cleanup, reload, truncate, or manual restore. |
| Manage volumes, folders, and files | User cancels upload/delete/clean/link exposure/workflow trigger or upload/trigger fails after a file is created. | Current uploaded file, folder, signed link visibility, or trigger handle only when scoped by typed results. | File/folder info, upload preview, refs, download/preview state, delete/clean read-back, and workflow trigger result. | Do not delete unrelated files, clean folders broadly, revoke unknown links, or claim workflow trigger rollback. | Return file/folder/trigger state and ask before deleting the created file, cleaning a folder, or canceling triggered work. |
| Deploy, run, inspect, rerun, and cancel workflows | User aborts deploy/run/rerun/cancel/delete, run start fails after an execution id exists, or cancel target changes. | Specific workflow run/rerun/cancel target; deployed workflow definitions only by explicit delete/update. | Workflow detail, run/node/rerun status, branch state, cancel result, and terminal/read-back state. | Do not delete workflows, cancel sibling runs, rerun automatically, or claim external node side effects rolled back. | Return workflow/run handles and terminal or partial status; ask before cancel, delete, rerun, or manual compensation. |
| Design workflow custom operators and templates | User aborts create/update/test/enable/disable/delete, test fails after execution, or validation shows unsafe code. | Current operator/template lifecycle change or test run result; code side effects are not silently cleaned. | Operator/template detail, code/schema validation, test output/status, enabled state, and delete/update read-back. | Do not delete templates/operators, disable code, or rewrite schemas as an automatic rollback. | Return validation/test/lifecycle state and ask before disabling, deleting, restoring code, or rerunning tests. |
| Publish and invoke WorkItem API services | User aborts publish/enable/disable/invoke, invocation times out, or publish succeeds but enable/invoke fails. | Specific service publish/enabled state or invocation request/result; payload side effects remain caller-owned. | Service info, publish status, enabled state, invocation status/result, timeout, and rate-limit evidence. | Do not unpublish services, disable endpoints, or replay/undo invocation payloads automatically. | Return service/invocation state and ask before disable/unpublish, repeated invoke, or payload-level compensation. |
| Review lineage artifacts and rerun from nodes/blocks | User aborts revision switch/rerun/exposure, rerun creates a branch, or switch target becomes stale. | Specific revision switch or rerun branch handle; artifact contents are not deleted as rollback. | Artifact overview, node/block/revision detail, effective revision, output summary, and rerun status. | Do not delete artifact blocks, switch back blindly, or cancel unrelated rerun branches. | Return lineage handles, effective revision, and branch status; ask before switching revision or canceling/deleting rerun output. |
| Manage semantic models and entries | User aborts create/update/delete/import/export, validation fails after import/export starts, or target becomes stale. | Specific model/entry/import/export result when represented by typed handles; semantic data movement is not silently reversed. | Model/entry detail, validation result, import/export result, list/search result, and lifecycle read-back. | Do not delete models, restore overwritten entries, or reimport/export as a rollback without approval. | Return semantic handles, validation/import/export state, and ask before delete, restore, reimport, or compensating export. |
| Manage connectors plus import/export tasks | User aborts connector update/delete, credential use, import/export start, pause/resume/retry/delete, or external transfer. | Specific connector file/task/transfer state; external source/target side effects require explicit compensation. | Connector info/source schema, credential validation result, MO target check, file preview, task state, runs, and files. | Do not delete connectors, revoke credentials, retry/resume/delete tasks, or erase target tables automatically. | Return connector/task/file state plus remaining external effects; ask before cleanup, retry, resume, delete, or target compensation. |
| Use product agents through A2A | User aborts send/stream/cancel/input-submit, task enters input-required, or duplicate request is detected. | Specific request/message/task/cancel/input turn represented by typed ids; agent tool side effects may remain. | Agent card, request/message ids, task state, input-required turn, stream/resubscribe state, and cancel result. | Do not generate cancel/input answers, delete task history, or claim agent tool calls were rolled back. | Return agent/task state and ask before cancel, input-submit, duplicate send, or tool-side compensation. |
| Configure model providers and routers | User aborts backend/router update, probe, secret update, delete, or external provider call. | Specific backend/router/endpoint/probe state; secrets and external calls are not automatically reversed. | Provider/backend/router detail, endpoint validation, probe result, model list, and redacted secret update state. | Do not delete backends, restore secrets from logs, change routers back, or hide provider probe side effects. | Return provider/router/probe state with secrets redacted and ask before delete, router restore, or secret rotation. |
| Manage compute resources | User aborts create/update/action/delete/force-delete, preflight fails, or action creates partial capacity state. | Specific compute resource/action/preflight result; runtime cost and external workloads are not silently reversed. | Resource detail, spec/image reads, preflight result, action status window, and list/delete read-back. | Do not force delete, resize back, suspend/resume, or retry resources automatically after partial action. | Return resource/action/preflight state and ask before force delete, resize restore, retry, or workload compensation. |
| Publish and subscribe data shares | User aborts publish/subscribe/unsubscribe/delete, target workspace choice changes, or governance check fails. | Specific publish/subscription/name-check result; cross-workspace exposure may require explicit revoke/delete. | Workspace summary, name-check, publish/subscription detail, target workspace list, object read, and governance state. | Do not unsubscribe/delete shares broadly, republish with guessed targets, or claim data exposure was undone. | Return share/subscription state and ask before revoke, delete, unsubscribe, or compensating governance action. |
| Work in the SQL editor and query history | User aborts execute/rerun/cancel/drop/kill/DDL/DML, query partially runs, or result exposure is declined. | Specific query/statement/workbook state; database mutations cannot be automatically rolled back unless product transaction evidence exists. | SQL text review, metadata, query/statement status, history, workbook/version detail, result/profile, and cancel/drop read-back. | Do not rewrite SQL, auto-run inverse SQL, drop/kill adjacent statements, or claim DDL/DML rollback without proof. | Return SQL/query/statement status plus remaining database side effects; ask before cancel, compensating SQL, delete, or rerun. |

### User Story Progress Visibility Coverage

Use this table after Abort and Cleanup Coverage and before Diagnostic Handoff Coverage so AI-written code reports real SDK progress instead of sleeping silently, polling forever, hiding partial stream/page/batch state, or claiming completion from a request id. Every promoted task must state progress signal, status/read call, user-visible update, forbidden silent wait, and AI-safe progress reporting. Code entries in progress visibility cells must be backed by the User Story Matrix, Flow Recipe, or Outcome Contract for the matched task. If progress cannot be observed, code must return the last typed handle/status/result plus the configured wait budget and stop instead of fabricating progress.

| User task | Progress signal | Status/read call | User-visible update | Forbidden silent wait | AI-safe progress reporting |
|-----------|-----------------|------------------|---------------------|-----------------------|----------------------------|
| Bootstrap a UI workspace and inspect identity | Authenticated workspace lookup result, create/ensure result, and DB connection availability. | workspace list/read, `EnsureWorkspace`, and DB connection read when requested. | Show workspace lookup status, unique workspace id/name, and DB connection readiness when authorized. | Do not wait silently on auth failures, duplicate workspace names, or missing endpoint/profile data. | Report current auth/workspace step, typed workspace handle or ambiguity, request id/error, and the next required user input. |
| Manage workspace members | Member detail, binding/status read-back, supported general audit results, and explicit permission-denied results. | Member handle detail/list/status operations, `IAMService.UserRoleBindings` / `user_role_bindings`, and `WorkspaceMemberService.AuditLogs`. | Show member id, requested action, read-back state, and permitted audit summary. | Do not hide propagation, retry denied calls without state, or wait for audit results without a budget. | Report scoped ids, current IAM binding/status evidence, audit availability, and any missing IAM action. |
| Browse catalog resources and operate tables | Catalog/database/table list, create/load/truncate/delete status, preview/data rows, ref list, and download availability. | Catalog/database/table info/list/data/preview/download/ref/status calls. | Show table path, schema/status summary, row/page count, load/delete/truncate state, and next page/ref availability. | Do not sleep after load/truncate/delete, assume preview means load completion, or hide empty pages. | Report typed table handle, current operation status, page/preview counts, and stop with last observed state when not terminal. |
| Manage volumes, folders, and files | Upload preview, file/folder info, download/preview links, ref list, workflow trigger result, and delete/clean result. | Volume/folder/file list/info/preview/download/ref/trigger read calls. | Show file/folder id/name, upload/preview availability, trigger status, ref count, and delete/clean read-back. | Do not wait silently for preview/link/trigger creation, hide missing refs, or claim delete without read-back. | Report typed file/folder handle, preview/link/trigger state, request id/error, and caller-visible next action. |
| Deploy, run, inspect, rerun, and cancel workflows | Workflow deployment state, execution status/result, node detail, rerun branch/status, cancel result, and terminal state. | Workflow/run/node/rerun get/status/result/list/cancel read calls. | Show workflow/run/node ids, current status, terminal/result summary, rerun branch, and cancel progress. | Do not poll forever, treat execution id as completion, hide node/rerun status, or claim cancel succeeded without read-back. | Report current handle/status, poll budget, terminal or partial state, and request id/error for the stuck step. |
| Design workflow custom operators and templates | Operator/template list/detail, code/test result, validation status, enabled state, and delete/update read-back. | Operator/template get/list/code/test/update/enable/disable/delete read calls. | Show operator/template id/name, validation/test status, enabled state, and lifecycle read-back. | Do not wait silently for tests, hide validation errors, or mark code enabled without state read. | Report typed design handle, test/validation summary, lifecycle state, and ask before exposing code/payload details. |
| Publish and invoke WorkItem API services | Service info, publish state, enabled state, invoke result/status, timeout, concurrency, and rate-limit visibility. | Service info/publish/enable/disable/invoke/status reads. | Show node/version/service id, published/enabled state, invocation status/result summary, and timeout/rate-limit state. | Do not hide invoke timeout, treat publish as invoke completion, or retry service calls without showing status. | Report service handle, current lifecycle/invoke state, wait budget, and redacted result/error summary. |
| Review lineage artifacts and rerun from nodes/blocks | Artifact overview, output block/revision list, effective revision switch, node detail, rerun status, and branch state. | Artifact/block/node/node-block/revision/rerun get/list/status calls. | Show artifact/node/block ids, revision/effective state, output summary, rerun status, and branch progress. | Do not wait silently for rerun, hide revision switch state, or claim output freshness from stale revision ids. | Report typed lineage handles, selected revision/switch, rerun status, and last observed output summary. |
| Manage semantic models and entries | Model/entry list/detail, validation result, import/export result, delete/update read-back, and empty list state. | Semantic model/entry get/list/validate/import/export/update/delete calls. | Show model/entry ids, validation/import/export status, count/page summary, and lifecycle read-back. | Do not hide validation/import/export progress, assume import success without result, or wait without status evidence. | Report typed semantic handles, validation/result summary, page/list state, and required user correction. |
| Manage connectors plus import/export tasks | Connector/source browse, file preview/download/stream state, task status, run list, file list, pause/resume/retry state. | Connector/task/file get/list/state/runs/files/preview/download/stream reads. | Show connector/task/file ids, current transfer state, run/file counts, source browse status, and retry/resume outcome. | Do not wait silently for transfers, retry without showing task state, or hide external dependency state. | Report task handle, transfer status, run/file progress, wait budget, and redacted external error or user decision. |
| Use product agents through A2A | Agent card, send acceptance, stream chunks, task status, resubscribe state, cancel result, and input-required state. | Agent card, send/stream response, `GetTask`, resubscribe, cancel, and input-submit calls. | Show request/message/task ids, method, stream/task state, input-required turn, cancel state, and final message status. | Do not wait silently for agent turns, treat send acceptance as final, generate new ids to check progress, or hide input-required state. | Report selector and request/message/task ids, current task state, stream/final status, and required input without exposing prompts/tool output. |
| Configure model providers and routers | Backend/endpoint/router detail, create/update/delete read-back, probe status, model list, and router strategy state. | Provider/backend/endpoint/router get/list/probe/update reads. | Show provider family, backend/router ids, probe/router status, model availability, and lifecycle read-back. | Do not wait silently on provider probes, hide endpoint errors, or claim router update without read-back. | Report typed provider handles, probe/router state, request id/error, and redacted missing config. |
| Manage compute resources | Resource get/update/preflight/action state, suspend/resume/retry result window, delete state, and cost/capacity status. | Compute resource get/update/preflight/action/delete reads. | Show resource id/name, current state, preflight warnings, action status window summary, and delete progress. | Do not wait silently on action/delete, ignore failed preflight, or claim capacity changes from request acceptance. | Report resource handle, action/preflight state, wait budget, and explicit approval needed for force/cost changes. |
| Publish and subscribe data shares | Workspace summary, name-check, publish/subscription detail, subscribe/unsubscribe/delete read-back, and governance state. | Data-share summary/name-check/publish/subscription get/list/update/delete reads. | Show publish/subscription ids, target/object scope, name availability, subscription state, and governance summary. | Do not wait silently for share visibility, hide target mismatch, or claim subscribe/delete without read-back. | Report share handles, current publish/subscription state, name-check result, and required target/object confirmation. |
| Work in the SQL editor and query history | Metadata browse, workbook/version state, query status, statement result/detail/profile, history page, cancel/kill/drop state. | SQL metadata/workbook/version/query/statement status/result/detail/profile/history reads. | Show workbook/query/statement ids, execution state, result/page/profile summary, history count, and cancel/drop read-back. | Do not wait silently for SQL execution, treat query id as result, rerun SQL without status, or hide pagination/profile state. | Report typed SQL handles, query/statement status, page/result counts, wait budget, and ask before exposing SQL/results. |

### User Story Diagnostic Handoff Coverage

Use this table after Progress Visibility Coverage and before Stability Evidence so AI-written code hands failed, stuck, or ambiguous SDK work to a user, support engineer, log search, or later AI step with enough typed context to diagnose the problem without leaking secrets, raw payloads, or unrelated data. Every promoted task must state safe diagnostic context, correlation/status handle, sensitive diagnostic boundary, forbidden diagnostic shortcut, and AI-safe diagnostic handoff. Code entries in diagnostic handoff cells must be backed by the User Story Matrix, Flow Recipe, or Outcome Contract for the matched task. If diagnostic evidence is unavailable, code must return the explicit error, request id when present, task/status handle when present, and the reviewed input summary instead of fabricating trace data or hiding the failure.

| User task | Safe diagnostic context | Correlation/status handle | Sensitive diagnostic boundary | Forbidden diagnostic shortcut | AI-safe diagnostic handoff |
|-----------|-------------------------|---------------------------|-------------------------------|-------------------------------|----------------------------|
| Bootstrap a UI workspace and inspect identity | Endpoint hostname, auth mode, account profile status, workspace id/name, and duplicate-name count are safe context. | SDK error code/request id, `WorkspaceHandle`, account profile result, workspace list result, and DB connection status. | API keys, tokens, session cookies, DB credentials, and raw auth headers remain secret. | Do not paste credentials, claim a workspace was selected without a unique typed match, or hide duplicate workspace evidence. | Return redacted endpoint/auth mode, error/request id, workspace lookup result, and the exact missing or ambiguous field. |
| Manage workspace members | Workspace id, member id, canonical role ids, target status, and a redacted audit summary are safe context. | SDK error/request id, `WorkspaceMemberHandle`, typed member result, `IAMService.UserRoleBindings` / `user_role_bindings` result, and permitted general audit id/time. | Auth context, unrelated members, contact data, denial internals, and policy traces remain restricted. | Do not expose raw policy blobs or use another audit row as proof. | Return scoped ids, requested member change, current IAM binding/read-back status, and redacted SDK error context. |
| Browse catalog resources and operate tables | Catalog/database/table ids, schema names, page limits, preview status, and load/truncate/delete target are safe context. | SDK error code/request id, catalog/database/table handles, load/status result, preview/download result, and list row. | Signed URLs, row samples, file URLs, credentials, and full data payloads follow caller visibility policy. | Do not dump raw rows, signed links, or storage URLs, and do not diagnose from display labels alone. | Return typed table path, operation name, status/read-back result, request id, and redacted sample counts or schema summary. |
| Manage volumes, folders, and files | Volume/folder/file ids, file name, size, preview type, upload status, and trigger target are safe context. | SDK error code/request id, volume/folder/file handles, upload preview, download/preview result, and trigger result. | Local filesystem paths, file bytes, signed links, connector temp content, and workflow inputs remain caller-controlled. | Do not log file bytes, leak local paths, paste signed URLs broadly, or hide failed delete/trigger state. | Return file/folder ids, operation, preview/download availability, request id, and redacted caller-visible file summary. |
| Deploy, run, inspect, rerun, and cancel workflows | Workflow id/name, execution id, node key, run status, rerun id, and cancel target are safe context. | SDK error code/request id, workflow/run/node/rerun handles, run result/status, node detail, and branch state. | Workflow DSL, run inputs, node logs, traces, and error payloads may contain secrets or user data. | Do not paste raw trace/log/input payloads, claim final status from a request id, or omit the run/node id. | Return workflow/run/node ids, status/result summary, request id, and redacted error context with logs only on caller approval. |
| Design workflow custom operators and templates | Operator/template id/name/version, enabled state, validation/test status, and lifecycle action are safe context. | SDK error code/request id, operator/template handles, test result, info/code metadata, and list result. | Operator code, schemas, test payloads, template DSL, and generated errors are caller-owned design artifacts. | Do not paste code/test payloads by default, mark unreviewed code safe, or hide enable/delete state. | Return ids, action, validation/test summary, request id, and ask before sharing code/schema/payload diagnostics. |
| Publish and invoke WorkItem API services | Node id, version, service name/type, enabled state, publish status, and invocation status are safe context. | SDK error code/request id, service handle, service info, publish/enable state, and invoke result id/status. | Service payloads/results, compute limits, and caller data inside invocation remain restricted. | Do not log payloads/results by default, treat enable success as invoke success, or omit node/version. | Return node/version/service ids, lifecycle state, request id, and redacted invoke summary with payload sharing only by approval. |
| Review lineage artifacts and rerun from nodes/blocks | Artifact id, node id, block id, revision id, effective revision, and rerun target are safe context. | SDK error code/request id, artifact/block/node handles, revision list, effective switch, and rerun status. | Artifact outputs, derived data, revision content, and lineage blobs may contain user data. | Do not paste raw artifact blocks, infer hidden revisions, or diagnose rerun from guessed ids. | Return lineage ids, selected revision/switch, rerun status, request id, and redacted output summary. |
| Manage semantic models and entries | Model id, entry id/kind, validation status, import/export action, and list state are safe context. | SDK error code/request id, model/entry handles, validation result, import/export result, and list/detail result. | Entry specs, model content, import/export payloads, and semantic definitions are caller-owned. | Do not paste full specs by default, duplicate entry content into logs, or hide delete/import/export failure. | Return model/entry ids, action, validation/import/export summary, request id, and ask before sharing specs. |
| Manage connectors plus import/export tasks | Connector id, source type, task id, file id, run id, task state, and transfer action are safe context. | SDK error code/request id, connector/task/file handles, source browse result, task info/state, runs, and files. | Credentials, storage config, source schemas, file contents, and external system errors may be sensitive. | Do not paste credentials/config, retry without state, or hide external dependency failure details from the caller. | Return connector/task/run ids, state summary, request id, redacted external error, and required user decision. |
| Use product agents through A2A | Agent selector, request id, message id, task id, turn id, method, and task status are safe context. | SDK error code/request id, agent handle, A2A task request, send/stream response, `GetTask`, and resubscribe status. | Prompts, tool inputs/outputs, retrieved context, and streamed content may contain user data. | Do not generate replacement request ids for diagnosis, paste tool output by default, or claim send acceptance as final task state. | Return selector, request/message/task ids, method/status, SDK error, and ask before sharing prompts or tool output. |
| Configure model providers and routers | Provider family, backend id/name, endpoint id, router id, model names, probe status, and action are safe context. | SDK error code/request id, provider/backend/router handles, endpoint status, probe result, and router read-back. | Secrets, endpoint credentials, API keys, provider error bodies, and model auth details remain secret. | Do not print secrets, paste full provider errors, or diagnose routing from unsourced model names. | Return provider/backend/router ids, redacted probe/router status, request id, and the exact missing config field. |
| Manage compute resources | Resource id/name, spec/image id, action, preflight status window, and current state are safe context. | SDK error code/request id, compute resource handle, preflight result, action status result, and list state. |, runtime logs, capacity, worker images, and failure logs may reveal operational internals. | Do not paste raw runtime logs, force-delete diagnostics, or hide failed preflight warnings. | Return resource/action ids, state/preflight summary, request id, and ask before sharing logs or forcing action. |
| Publish and subscribe data shares | Publish id, subscription id, object id, target workspace id, name-check result, and action are safe context. | SDK error code/request id, publish/subscription handles, summary, name availability, and subscribe/list state. | Shared object names, target workspaces, subscriber metadata, and governance context may be sensitive. | Do not expose unauthorized target workspaces, hide unsubscribe/delete failure, or infer share target. | Return share ids, action/status, governance summary, request id, and required target/object confirmation. |
| Work in the SQL editor and query history | Workbook id, version id, query id, statement id, database/table name, status, and result limit are safe context. | SDK error code/request id, workbook/version/query/statement handles, query status, statement result/detail/profile, and metadata read. | SQL text, result rows, profiles, connection errors, and DDL/DML content may contain sensitive data. | Do not log SQL/results by default, paste profiles broadly, or treat query id as result data. | Return workbook/query/statement ids, status/profile summary, request id, and ask before sharing SQL text or rows. |

### User Story Freshness and Conflict Coverage

Use this table after Diagnostic Handoff Coverage and before Stability Evidence so AI-written code does not update, delete, cancel, rerun, publish, subscribe, or execute against stale list results, cached handles, superseded revisions, or concurrently changed resources. Every promoted task must state fresh-state source, conflict/stale signal, destructive/update conflict boundary, forbidden stale-state shortcut, and AI-safe conflict handling. Code entries in freshness/conflict cells must be backed by the User Story Matrix, Flow Recipe, or Outcome Contract for the matched task. If freshness cannot be proven, code must re-read the typed resource or stop for explicit user confirmation instead of overwriting, deleting, canceling, rerunning, or publishing from stale state.

| User task | Fresh-state source | Conflict/stale signal | Destructive/update conflict boundary | Forbidden stale-state shortcut | AI-safe conflict handling |
|-----------|--------------------|-----------------------|--------------------------------------|-------------------------------|---------------------------|
| Bootstrap a UI workspace and inspect identity | Fresh account profile, workspace list, unique `EnsureWorkspace` result, or explicit workspace id selection. | Duplicate workspace names, missing workspace after create, changed workspace status, or auth/session error. | Workspace update/delete or DB connection use must be based on a current workspace read. | Do not reuse a stale workspace name match, pick the first duplicate, or continue after auth context changes. | Re-read account/workspace state, bind the exact id returned or selected, and ask when duplicate or changed state appears. |
| Manage workspace members | Fresh member detail, canonical bindings, status, and supported general audit results. | Status or bindings changed, member deleted, permission denied, or read-back contradicts expected state. | Binding/status/delete mutations must use current member state. | Do not apply cached binding lists, overwrite concurrent changes, or delete from a stale member handle. | Re-read the member before mutation, return conflict evidence, and ask before applying a changed decision. |
| Browse catalog resources and operate tables | Fresh catalog/database/table info, children/list result, schema read, load status, and preview/status read. | Table/schema/status changed, table missing, load still running, truncate/delete already happened, or name conflict. | Create/load/truncate/delete must use current table identity and schema/status. | Do not load or truncate based on old previews, cached schema, or display names. | Re-read table info/status/schema, compare against caller-selected target, and ask before destructive or schema-changing work. |
| Manage volumes, folders, and files | Fresh volume/folder/file info, list/ref-list result, upload preview, and trigger result. | File/folder renamed, deleted, replaced, ref list changed, upload preview changed, or trigger target changed. | Rename/delete/clean/upload replacement/trigger must use current file or folder state. | Do not delete or trigger from stale file ids, old local paths, or cached signed links. | Re-read file/folder state, verify id/name/ref match the user target, and ask before replacing or deleting changed content. |
| Deploy, run, inspect, rerun, and cancel workflows | Fresh workflow info, run status/result, node detail, rerun branch/info, and cancel status. | Workflow updated, run moved terminal, node key missing, rerun branch changed, cancel already applied, or run status conflicts. | Run/rerun/cancel/delete must be based on current workflow/run/node/rerun state. | Do not cancel or rerun from stale run status, old node detail, or previous branch lists. | Refresh workflow/run/node/rerun state, return terminal/conflict status, and ask before starting another run or canceling. |
| Design workflow custom operators and templates | Fresh operator/template info, code metadata, enabled state, test result, and list/read-back. | Code/version/enabled state changed, template missing, test result stale, or concurrent update/delete detected by read-back. | Update/enable/disable/delete/test must use the current operator/template state. | Do not overwrite changed code, enable stale test results, or delete from cached list entries. | Re-read design state, compare id/name/version/enabled state, and ask before overwriting, enabling, disabling, or deleting. |
| Publish and invoke WorkItem API services | Fresh service info, node/version metadata, enabled state, publish result, and invoke-readiness status. | Node/version changed, service already published/disabled, limits changed, invoke payload version mismatch, or service missing. | Publish/enable/disable/invoke must use current node/version/service state. | Do not invoke against stale service metadata, publish duplicate versions, or assume enable state from old reads. | Get service info before lifecycle changes, compare version and enabled state, and ask before republish or repeated invocation. |
| Review lineage artifacts and rerun from nodes/blocks | Fresh artifact overview, block/node output, revision list, effective switch, and rerun status. | Revision changed, effective switch changed, block/node missing, rerun target no longer valid, or output list changed. | Revision switch/create-rerun must use current artifact/node/block/revision state. | Do not switch or rerun from stale revision lists, guessed block ids, or old effective revision output. | Re-read lineage and effective revisions, return conflict state, and ask before switch or rerun. |
| Manage semantic models and entries | Fresh model info, entry list/detail, validation result, import/export result, and delete/list read-back. | Entry/model changed or deleted, validation result stale, import/export changed entries, or duplicate entry appears. | Entry update/delete/import/export must use current model/entry state. | Do not overwrite changed entry specs, delete from stale lists, or import/export after unreviewed changes. | Re-read model/entry state, validate current specs, and ask before overwrite, delete, import, or export. |
| Manage connectors plus import/export tasks | Fresh connector info/source metadata, task info/state, files/runs, and file preview/download state. | Connector config changed, task state moved, files changed, run failed/completed, external source schema changed, or credentials invalid. | Update/delete/pause/resume/retry/import/export must use current connector/task/file state. | Do not retry/resume/delete from stale task state or reuse old source schemas/config. | Inspect current task/source state, return conflict/terminal state, and ask before retry/resume/delete or new transfer. |
| Use product agents through A2A | Fresh agent card, task status, turn state, send/stream response, and resubscribe status. | Task already terminal, turn id changed, request id reused for different payload, selector changed, or stream status changed. | Send/stream/cancel/input-submit must use current task/turn/request state. | Do not submit input to stale turn ids, cancel terminal tasks, or mint new ids to mask a conflict. | Get task/status first, reuse the correct request id for the same action, and ask before duplicate send/cancel/input-submit. |
| Configure model providers and routers | Fresh provider/backend/router info, endpoint status, model list/probe result, and delete/list read-back. | Backend/router changed, endpoint status changed, model list stale, secret rotated, or provider deleted. | Update/probe/router change/delete must use current provider/backend/router state. | Do not overwrite router config, delete stale backend ids, or trust old probe results. | Re-read provider/backend/router state, compare model/endpoint/router target, and ask before update/probe/delete. |
| Manage compute resources | Fresh resource detail, spec/image list, preflight result, action status window, and list read-back. | Resource state changed, action already running/terminal, spec/image changed, preflight warnings changed, or window stale. | Update/action/delete must use current resource state and preflight result. | Do not resize, suspend/resume, retry, or force delete from stale or old preflight. | Re-read resource/preflight/action state, return current status, and ask before cost or destructive action. |
| Publish and subscribe data shares | Fresh summary, publication/subscription info/list, object list, target workspace state, and name availability. | Publication/subscription changed, name availability changed, object/target workspace changed, or governance status changed. | Publish/update/delete/subscribe/unsubscribe must use current share/object/target state. | Do not publish from old name checks, subscribe to changed targets, or delete stale share ids. | Re-read share summary/name/object/target state, return conflicts, and ask before publish/subscribe/delete. |
| Work in the SQL editor and query history | Fresh workbook/version detail, metadata read, query status, statement result/detail/profile, and history read. | Workbook/version changed, query status moved terminal, statement result changed, metadata changed, or database/table dropped. | Save/execute/cancel/drop/kill must use current workbook/query/statement/metadata state. | Do not overwrite workbook versions, rerun stale SQL, cancel terminal queries, or drop from cached metadata. | Re-read workbook/version/query/metadata state, return current status, and ask before overwrite, execute, cancel, drop, or kill. |

### User Story Stability Evidence

Use this table to audit whether a promoted user-story task is stable enough for users and AI-written code. Every task in the User Story Matrix must name the Go and Python facade tests that prove handle scoping, typed/spec entry points, and the expected failure boundary. If a task cannot name both tests, keep it route-aligned instead of promoting it.

| User task | Go facade tests | Python facade tests | Stable proof | Failure boundary |
|-----------|-----------------|---------------------|--------------|------------------|
| Bootstrap a UI workspace and inspect identity | `TestEnsureWorkspaceFindsExistingWorkspace`; `TestEnsureWorkspaceCreatesMissingWorkspace`; `TestEnsureWorkspaceRejectsDuplicateWorkspaceName`; `TestEnsureWorkspaceFindsExistingWorkspace` | `test_ensure_workspace_and_scoped_handle`; `test_ensure_workspace_rejects_duplicate_workspace_name` | Proves product endpoint bootstrap returns name-resolved workspace handle, known-id workspace handle, and database connection through typed helpers. | Duplicate workspace names, missing endpoint/auth, and invalid workspace ids must fail explicitly instead of selecting an arbitrary resource. |
| Manage workspace members | `TestWorkspaceMemberHandleScopesRequests` | `test_workspace_member_handle_scopes_requests` | Proves invitation/member lifecycle calls retain workspace/member scope while the same facade test exercises canonical IAM role-binding GET/PUT with explicit CAS. | Active member lifecycle calls require accepted membership; missing member, canonical role ids, or binding versions must be supplied from typed IAM results, UI state, or explicit input. |
| Browse catalog resources and operate tables | `TestWorkspaceCatalogBrowseHandlesScopeDiscoveredResources`; `TestWorkspaceCatalogTableHandleScopesDatabaseAndTable`; `TestWorkspaceCatalogTableHandleValidatesRequiredArguments`; `TestWorkspaceHandlePrepareCatalogCreatesMissingCatalog` | `test_workspace_catalog_browse_handles_scope_discovered_resources`; `test_workspace_catalog_table_handle_scopes_database_and_table`; `test_ensure_workspace_and_scoped_handle` | Proves catalog/database/table handles can be discovered or created before table read/write operations. | Catalog, database, table, column definitions, and file URLs must come from typed catalog results or reviewed caller input. |
| Manage volumes, folders, and files | `TestWorkspaceHandlePreparesCatalogAndDatabase`; `TestDatabaseHandlePrepareVolumeCreatesMissingVolume`; `TestWorkspaceFolderHandleScopesVolumeFolderLifecycle`; `TestWorkspaceFileHandleScopesVolumeFileLifecycle`; `TestWorkspaceConnectorFileHandleScopesTemporaryFileLifecycle` | `test_ensure_workspace_and_scoped_handle`; `test_workspace_folder_handle_scopes_volume_folder_lifecycle`; `test_workspace_file_handle_scopes_volume_file_lifecycle`; `test_workspace_connector_file_handle_scopes_temporary_file_lifecycle` | Proves volume, folder, file, upload preview, and temporary connector-file handles carry scope through lifecycle calls. | File bytes/paths and volume/folder/file ids must come from upload/create/list results, UI state, or explicit input. |
| Deploy, run, inspect, rerun, and cancel workflows | `TestWorkspaceWorkflowHandleScopesLifecycle` | `test_workspace_workflow_handle_scopes_lifecycle` | Proves workflow, run, node, and rerun handles scope deploy/run/result/retry/cancel flows. | Workflow DSL, run ids, node keys, rerun ids, and rerun choices must come from reviewed workflow input, backend results, or UI state. |
| Design workflow custom operators and templates | `TestWorkspaceWorkflowDesignHandlesScopeOperatorsAndTemplates` | `test_workspace_workflow_design_handles_scope_operators_and_templates` | Proves custom-operator specs/test input and template handles cover create/test/update/enable/disable/delete flows. | Operator/template ids, schemas, code, DSL, and payloads must come from reviewed design input, create/list/get results, or explicit input. |
| Publish and invoke WorkItem API services | `TestWorkspaceWorkitemAPIServiceHandleScopesLifecycle` | `test_workspace_workitem_api_service_handle_scopes_lifecycle` | Proves versioned WorkItem API service handles publish, enable, invoke, and disable one catalog node/version. | Node id, version, service name/type, and invoke payload must come from WorkItem metadata, UI state, or explicit input. |
| Review lineage artifacts and rerun from nodes/blocks | `TestWorkspaceArtifactHandlesScopeLineageReview`; `TestWorkspaceArtifactHandlesValidateRequiredArguments` | `test_workspace_artifact_handles_scope_lineage_review` | Proves artifact, block, node, and node-block handles inspect lineage outputs and create reruns. | Artifact, node, block, revision, and output emission ids must come from lineage/run results, UI state, or explicit input. |
| Manage semantic models and entries | `TestWorkspaceSemanticModelHandleScopesEntries` | `test_workspace_semantic_model_handle_scopes_entries` | Proves semantic model and entry handles cover entry lifecycle, validation, import, and export. | Model/entry ids and entry specs must come from create/list/get results, semantic editor state, reviewed input, or explicit input. |
| Manage connectors plus import/export tasks | `TestWorkspaceConnectorHandleScopesTasks` | `test_workspace_connector_handle_scopes_tasks` | Proves connector handles, file import specs, import task handles, export specs, and export task handles cover task lifecycle. | Connector credentials, storage config, volume ids, file ids, task ids, and enum values must come from metadata/results, secret storage, UI state, or explicit input. |
| Use product agents through A2A | `TestWorkspaceAgentHandleScopesA2ASelector` | `test_workspace_agent_handle_scopes_a2a_selector` | Proves A2A agent handles build selector-scoped message, stream, task-control, resubscribe, and input-submit requests. | Request/message ids are caller-generated; task and turn ids must come from A2A results. Agent resource CRUD stays route-aligned. |
| Configure model providers and routers | `TestWorkspaceModelProviderHandleScopesBackendAndRouter` | `test_workspace_model_provider_handle_scopes_backend_and_router` | Proves provider/backend/router handles cover backend lifecycle, endpoint management, probing, and router updates. | Backend ids, endpoint addresses, model lists, secrets, and router strategy must come from config, secret storage, backend metadata, or explicit input. |
| Manage compute resources | `TestWorkspaceComputeResourceHandleScopesLifecycle` | `test_workspace_compute_resource_handle_scopes_lifecycle` | Proves compute resource handles cover create/get/update/preflight/action/delete. | Resource ids, spec ids, sizing values, and delete decisions must come from compute metadata/results, UI state, or explicit input. |
| Publish and subscribe data shares | `TestWorkspaceDataShareHandleScopesPublishAndSubscription` | `test_workspace_data_share_handle_scopes_publish_and_subscription` | Proves publish and subscription handles cover summary/check/update/delete/subscribe/unsubscribe flows. | Publish ids, subscription ids, object ids, locations, target workspaces, and share choices must come from product results, UI state, or explicit input. |
| Work in the SQL editor and query history | `TestWorkspaceSQLHandlesScopeWorkbookQueryAndStatement`; `TestWorkspaceSQLMetadataHandleScopesDatabaseAndTable` | `test_workspace_sql_handles_scope_workbook_query_and_statement`; `test_workspace_sql_metadata_handle_scopes_database_and_table` | Proves SQL metadata, database/table, workbook/version, query, and statement handles cover browsing, editing, execution, polling, cancellation, and result inspection. | Database/table names must come from metadata or UI state; workbook/query/statement ids must come from create/execute/history/detail results, not SQL text. |

### User Story Representative Method Coverage

Use this table to keep the promoted facade user-shaped instead of a route-by-route translation. Every task in the User Story Matrix must name representative Go and Python calls that appear in the language README examples. These calls are the minimum visible path for AI-generated code to identify the right high-level workflow before dropping to lower-level service methods.

| Manage workspace members | `workspace.InviteMember(ctx`<br>`workspace.Member(`<br>`workspace.IAM().UserRoleBindings(ctx`<br>`workspace.IAM().PutUserRoleBindings(ctx` | `workspace.invite_member(`<br>`workspace.member(`<br>`workspace.iam().user_role_bindings(`<br>`workspace.iam().put_user_role_bindings(` | README examples and facade tests must keep email/mobile pending invitation creation separate from active member lifecycle. GitHub immediate seat may bind a member handle from a non-empty result id; role mutations use the canonical IAM binding contract. |
| Browse catalog resources and operate tables | `workspace.PrepareCatalog(ctx`<br>`database.CreateTable(ctx`<br>`table.Data(ctx` | `workspace.prepare_catalog(`<br>`database.create_table_from_columns(`<br>`table.data(` | README examples and facade tests must show these calls as the minimal promoted path for this task. |
